EXECUTIVE READING

先给结论，再进入源码

核心机制

一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。

证据规模

27 条 finding / 27 处引用

主要边界

当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

适用建设

一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。

值得借鉴

  • 一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。
  • 模型说到一个完整动作就能开工，不必等整段回答结束；用户中途补充信息时，系统也能在安全位置收住并接新指令。
  • 同一轮尽量复用连接和粘性状态；行李箱塞不下不会盲目重拨网络，而是交给压缩逻辑处理。

需要警惕

  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

  • 当前页面复用既有源码账本；迁移到自研前仍需做部署级验证

00 · METHOD

研究口径：不是 README 摘要

README POLICY

README 只定位入口和产品命名；实现结论必须回到运行时代码、契约或测试。

FACT POLICY

L1 运行代码优先，L2 类型/事件契约补足，L3/L4 交叉验证，L5 推断单独标记。

模型与上下文verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

审计审批策略、Guardian/用户裁决、权限 profile 与 macOS/Linux/Windows sandbox 后端。

连接器、插件与 MCPverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

审计连接复用、OAuth、required server、工具过滤、catalog revision、elicitation 和超时。

全部指令与扩展面verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

审计 AGENTS.md 层级、skills 预算、plugin 注入和全生命周期 hooks。

协作与子 AgentverifiedL1 / L2 / L3

观测、日志与回放verifiedL1 / L2 / L3

审计 JSONL rollout、SQLite 镜像、日志/目标/记忆分库、tracing/OTEL 和工具时延。

恢复、测试与取舍verifiedL2 / L3

确认 Apache-2.0、源码规模与测试密度。

01
DIMENSION · ARCHITECTURE

架构与所有权

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factarchitecture

01 · 每个 turn 由多个 step 组成，step 内共享一次不可漂移的上下文快照

源码事实

run_turn 在采样前处理压缩、skills/plugins、session/input hooks 和待处理输入；随后为一个 step 捕获 StepContext，并用同一视图构造上下文、工具与模型请求。

白话解释

一轮任务可以问模型很多次，但每一次“想一想并行动”的小步都先拍一张现场快照，避免工具清单和提示词在同一步里前后不一致。

对自研 Harness 的含义

配置热更新只能在安全边界生效，换来 prompt cache 和工具调用的确定性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 153–212
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 213–274
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗

  153  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  154      sess: Arc<Session>,
  155      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  156      turn_extension_data: Arc<codex_extension_api::ExtensionData>,
  157      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  158      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  159      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  160  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  161      let mut client_session =
  162          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  163      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  164      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  165      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  166      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  167      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(
  168          &sess,
  169          &turn_context,
  170          &mut client_session,
  171          &cancellation_token,
  172      )
  173      .await
  174      {
  175          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) {
  176              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  177              return Err(err);
  178          }
  179          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  180          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  181              .await;
  182          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  183          return Ok(None);
  184      }
  185
  186      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  187      let first_step_context = match sess
  188          .capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  189          .await
  190      {
  191          Ok(step_context) => step_context,
  192          Err(err) if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) => {
  193              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  194              return Err(err);
  195          }
  196          Err(err) => return Err(err),
  197      };
  198      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  199      let (mut world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  200          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  201          turn_diff_display_roots(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  202      );
  203
  204      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  205          &sess,
  206          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  207          &input,
  208          &cancellation_token,
  209      )
  210      .await
  211      else {
  212          return Ok(None);
  213      };
  214
  215      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  216          return Ok(None);
  217      }
  218      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  219      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await {
  220          return Ok(None);
  221      }
  222
  223      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  224          .await;
  225      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  226          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  227          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  228          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  229      }))
  230      .await;
  231      for response_item in injection_items {
  232          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  233              .await;
  234      }
  235
  236      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  237
  238      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  239      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  240      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  241      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  242      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  243          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  244      ));
  245
  246      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  247      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  248      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  249      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  250      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  251      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  252
  253      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);
  254      loop {
  255          // Note that pending_input would be something like a message the user
  256          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI
  257          // may support this, the model might not.
  258          let pending_input = if can_drain_pending_input {
  259              sess.input_queue.get_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await
  260          } else {
  261              Vec::new()
  262          };
  263
  264          if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &pending_input).await {
  265              break;
  266          }
  267
  268          let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  269          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(
  270              sess.as_ref(),
  271              turn_context.as_ref(),
  272              &window_id,
  273          )
  274          .await;
  275
  276          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  277          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {
  278              Some(step_context) => step_context,
  279              None => {
  280                  sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  281                      .await?
  282              }
  283          };
  284          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  285              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  286                  sess.as_ref(),
  287                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  288                  &window_id,
  289              )
  290              .await?;
  291
  292              world_state = sess
  293                  .record_step_world_state_if_changed(&world_state, step_context.as_ref())
  294                  .await;
  295
  296              // Construct the input that we will send to the model.
  297              let sampling_request_input: Vec<ResponseItem> = async {
  298                  sess.clone_history()
  299                      .await
  300                      .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities)
  301              }
  302              .instrument(trace_span!("run_turn.prepare_sampling_request_input"))
  303              .await;
  304
  305              let responses_metadata = turn_context.turn_metadata_state.to_responses_metadata(
  306                  sess.installation_id.clone(),
  307                  window_id,
  308                  CodexResponsesRequestKind::Turn,
  309              );
  310              run_sampling_request(
  311                  Arc::clone(&sess),
  312                  Arc::clone(&step_context),
  313                  Arc::clone(&turn_extension_data),
  314                  Arc::clone(&turn_diff_tracker),
  315                  &mut client_session,
  316                  &responses_metadata,
  317                  sampling_request_input,
  318                  cancellation_token.child_token(),
  319              )
  320              .await
  321          }
  322          .await;
  323          match sampling_request_result {
  324              Ok((sampling_request_output, sampling_request_input)) => {
  325                  let SamplingRequestResult {
  326                      needs_follow_up: model_needs_follow_up,
  327                      last_agent_message: sampling_request_last_agent_message,
  328                  } = sampling_request_output;
  329                  if model_needs_follow_up {
  330                      sess.input_queue
  331                          .accept_mailbox_delivery_for_current_turn(
  332                              &sess.active_turn,
  333                              &turn_context.sub_id,
  334                          )
  335                          .await;
  336                  }
  337                  can_drain_pending_input = true;
  338                  let (has_pending_input, token_status) = async {
  339                      let has_pending_input =
  340                          sess.input_queue.has_pending_input(&sess.active_turn).await;
  341                      let token_status = super::context_window::context_window_token_status(
  342                          sess.as_ref(),
  343                          turn_context.as_ref(),
  344                      )
  345                      .await;

  153  pub(crate) async fn run_turn(
  154      sess: Arc<Session>,
  155      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  156      input: Vec<TurnInput>,
  157      prewarmed_client_session: Option<ModelClientSession>,
  158      cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
  159  ) -> CodexResult<Option<String>> {
  160      // Record results from hooks that finished after the previous turn before this turn's user prompt.
  161      drain_async_hook_results(&sess, &turn_context, /*before_user_prompt*/ true).await;
  162
  163      let mut client_session =
  164          prewarmed_client_session.unwrap_or_else(|| sess.services.model_client.new_session());
  165      // TODO(ccunningham): Pre-turn compaction runs before context updates and the
  166      // new user message are recorded. Estimate pending incoming items (context
  167      // diffs/full reinjection + user input) and trigger compaction preemptively
  168      // when they would push the thread over the compaction threshold.
  169      if let Err(err) = run_pre_sampling_compact(
  170          &sess,
  171          &turn_context,
  172          &mut client_session,
  173          &cancellation_token,
  174      )
  175      .await
  176      {
  177          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) {
  178              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  179                  .await;
  180              return Err(err);
  181          }
  182          if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::ToolCollision(_)) {
  183              return Err(err);
  184          }
  185          let error = err.to_codex_protocol_error();
  186          sess.emit_turn_error_lifecycle(turn_context.as_ref(), error.clone())
  187              .await;
  188          error!("Failed to run pre-sampling compact");
  189          return Ok(None);
  190      }
  191
  192      let user_input = turn_user_input(&input);
  193      let (required_servers, mentioned_plugins) =
  194          match required_mcp_servers_for_input(&sess, turn_context.as_ref(), &user_input)
  195              .or_cancel(&cancellation_token)
  196              .await
  197          {
  198              Ok(requirements) => requirements,
  199              Err(err) => {
  200                  run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  201                      .await;
  202                  return Err(err.into());
  203              }
  204          };
  205
  206      // run_turn owns the step used to seed context and make the first sampling request.
  207      let first_step_context = match sess
  208          .capture_step_context_with_required_mcp_servers(
  209              Arc::clone(&turn_context),
  210              &cancellation_token,
  211              &required_servers,
  212          )
  213          .await
  214      {
  215          Ok(step_context) => step_context,
  216          Err(err) if matches!(err.details(), CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted) => {
  217              run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::Standard)
  218                  .await;
  219              return Err(err);
  220          }
  221          Err(err) => return Err(err),
  222      };
  223      // Keep the exact model-visible state used by this turn and its inline compactions.
  224      let (world_state, display_roots) = tokio::join!(
  225          sess.record_context_updates_and_set_reference_context_item(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  226          turn_diff_display_roots(first_step_context.as_ref()),
  227      );
  228      let mut world_state = world_state?;
  229
  230      let Some((injection_items, explicitly_enabled_connectors)) = build_skills_and_plugins(
  231          &sess,
  232          first_step_context.as_ref(),
  233          &user_input,
  234          &mentioned_plugins,
  235          &cancellation_token,
  236      )
  237      .await
  238      else {
  239          return Ok(None);
  240      };
  241
  242      if run_pending_session_start_hooks(&sess, &turn_context).await {
  243          return Ok(None);
  244      }
  245      let mut can_drain_pending_input = input.is_empty();
  246      if run_hooks_and_record_inputs(&sess, &turn_context, &input, PersistContext::TurnStart).await {
  247          return Ok(None);
  248      }
  249
  250      sess.merge_connector_selection(explicitly_enabled_connectors.clone())
  251          .await;
  252      sess.set_previous_turn_settings(Some(PreviousTurnSettings {
  253          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  254          comp_hash: turn_context.model_info.comp_hash.clone(),
  255          realtime_active: Some(turn_context.realtime_active),
  256      }))
  257      .await;
  258      for response_item in injection_items {
  259          sess.record_conversation_items(&turn_context, std::slice::from_ref(&response_item))
  260              .await;
  261      }
  262
  263      track_turn_resolved_config_analytics(&sess, &turn_context, &input).await;
  264
  265      let mut last_agent_message: Option<String> = None;
  266      let mut stop_hook_active = false;
  267      // Although from the perspective of codex.rs, TurnDiffTracker has the lifecycle of a Task which contains
  268      // many turns, from the perspective of the user, it is a single turn.
  269      let turn_diff_tracker = Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
  270          TurnDiffTracker::with_environment_display_roots(display_roots),
  271      ));
  272
  273      // `ModelClientSession` is turn-scoped and caches WebSocket + sticky routing state, so we reuse
  274      // one instance across retries within this turn.
  275      // Pending input is drained into history before building the next model request.
  276      // However, we defer that drain until after sampling in two cases:
  277      // 1. At the start of a turn, so the fresh turn input in `input` gets sampled first.
  278      // 2. After auto-compact, when model/tool continuation needs to resume before any steer.
  279
  280      let mut next_step_context = Some(first_step_context);

  283          // submitted through the UI while the model was running. Though the UI

  289                  .0
  290          } else {

  302              break;
  303          }

  306          super::rollout_budget::maybe_record_reminder(

  308              turn_context.as_ref(),
  309              &window_id,
  310          )

  312
  313          // Capture once so context, advertised tools, and tool calls share one request view.
  314          let step_context = match next_step_context.take() {

  316              None if pending_input.is_empty() => {
  317                  sess.capture_step_context(Arc::clone(&turn_context), &cancellation_token)
  318                      .await?
  319              }
  320              None => {
  321                  let pending_user_input = turn_user_input(&pending_input);
  322                  let (required_servers, _) = required_mcp_servers_for_input(
  323                      &sess,
  324                      turn_context.as_ref(),
  325                      &pending_user_input,
  326                  )
  327                  .or_cancel(&cancellation_token)
  328                  .await?;
  329                  sess.capture_step_context_with_required_mcp_servers(
  330                      Arc::clone(&turn_context),
  331                      &cancellation_token,
  332                      &required_servers,
  333                  )
  334                  .await?
  335              }
  336          };
  337          let sampling_request_result: CodexResult<_> = async {
  338              super::time_reminder::maybe_record_current_time_reminder(
  339                  sess.as_ref(),
  340                  turn_context.as_ref(),
  341                  &window_id,
  342              )
  343              .await?;
  344
  345              world_state = sess
02
L1factarchitecture

02 · 流式采样与工具 Future 同时推进，并可被新消息抢占

源码事实

try_run_sampling_request 消费流式 ResponseEvent；完整 tool item 到达后进入 FuturesOrdered，工具参数 diff 可边流边展示；若 commentary/reasoning 期间 mailbox 有消息，则返回 needs_follow_up。

白话解释

模型说到一个完整动作就能开工，不必等整段回答结束；用户中途补充信息时，系统也能在安全位置收住并接新指令。

对自研 Harness 的含义

响应更快，但对调用顺序、取消、配对和幂等要求很高。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2034–2100
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2101–2168
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 2081      let mut active_item: Option<TurnItem> = None;
 2082      let mut active_tool_argument_diff_consumer: Option<(
 2083          String,
 2084          Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>,
 2085      )> = None;
 2086      let mut should_emit_turn_diff = false;
 2087      let mut should_emit_token_count = false;
 2088      let reasoning_effort = turn_context.effective_reasoning_effort_for_tracing();

03
L1factarchitecture

03 · 重试预算属于 turn-scoped client session，窗口超限不当作普通网络错误重试

源码事实

run_sampling_request 在一个 turn 内复用 ModelClientSession；上下文超限和 usage limit 直接上抛，其他可重试错误才按 Provider 的 stream retry 上限重试。

白话解释

同一轮尽量复用连接和粘性状态；行李箱塞不下不会盲目重拨网络，而是交给压缩逻辑处理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

把语义恢复与传输恢复分开，避免无效重试放大费用。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1176–1223
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1224–1273
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 1254              original_input = Some(prompt.input);
 1255          }

 1258              return Err(err);

 1298          let plugins_config = turn_context.config.plugins_config_input();
 1299          sess.services
 1300              .plugins_manager
 1301              .recommended_plugin_candidates_for_config(RecommendedPluginCandidatesInput {
 1302                  plugins_config: &plugins_config,
 1303                  loaded_plugins: &loaded_plugins,
 1304                  auth: auth.as_ref(),
 1305                  disabled_tools: &turn_context.config.tool_suggest.disabled_tools,
 1306                  app_server_client_name: turn_context.app_server_client_name.as_deref(),
 1307              })
 1308              .await
 1309      } else {
 1310          None
 1311      };
 1312
 1313      PreparedToolRecommendations {
 1314          auth,
 1315          endpoint_candidates,
 1316      }
 1317  }
 1318

 1320      skip_all,
 1321      fields(
 1322          turn_id = %turn_context.sub_id,
 1323          model = %turn_context.model_info.slug,
 1324          apps_enabled = turn_context.apps_enabled()
 1325      )
 1326  )]

 1343              connectors::with_app_enabled_state(connectors.clone(), &turn_context.config)
 1344          });

 1244
 1245  pub(super) fn collect_explicit_app_ids_from_skill_items(
 1246      skill_items: &[ResponseItem],
 1247      connectors: &[connectors::AppInfo],
 1248      skill_name_counts_lower: &HashMap<String, usize>,
 1249  ) -> HashSet<String> {
 1250      if skill_items.is_empty() || connectors.is_empty() {
 1251          return HashSet::new();
 1252      }
 1253
 1254      let skill_messages = skill_items
 1255          .iter()
 1256          .filter_map(|item| match item {
 1257              ResponseItem::Message { content, .. } => {
 1258                  content.iter().find_map(|content_item| match content_item {
 1259                      ContentItem::InputText { text } => Some(text.clone()),
 1260                      _ => None,
 1261                  })
 1262              }
 1263              _ => None,
 1264          })
 1265          .collect::<Vec<String>>();
 1266      if skill_messages.is_empty() {
 1267          return HashSet::new();
 1268      }
 1269
 1270      let mentions = collect_tool_mentions_from_messages(&skill_messages);
 1271      let mention_names_lower = mentions
 1272          .plain_names
 1273          .iter()
 1274          .map(|name| name.to_ascii_lowercase())
 1275          .collect::<HashSet<String>>();
 1276      let mut connector_ids = mentions
 1277          .paths
 1278          .iter()
 1279          .filter(|path| tool_kind_for_path(path) == ToolMentionKind::App)
 1280          .filter_map(|path| app_id_from_path(path).map(str::to_string))
 1281          .collect::<HashSet<String>>();
 1282
 1283      let connector_slug_counts = build_connector_slug_counts(connectors);
 1284      for connector in connectors {
 1285          let slug = codex_connectors::metadata::connector_mention_slug(connector);
 1286          let connector_count = connector_slug_counts.get(&slug).copied().unwrap_or(0);
 1287          let skill_count = skill_name_counts_lower.get(&slug).copied().unwrap_or(0);
 1288          if connector_count == 1 && skill_count == 0 && mention_names_lower.contains(&slug) {
 1289              connector_ids.insert(connector.id.clone());
 1290          }
 1291      }
 1292
 1293      connector_ids
 1294  }

 1307          base_instructions,
 1308          output_schema: turn_context.final_output_json_schema.clone(),
 1309          output_schema_strict: !crate::guardian::is_guardian_reviewer_source(
 1310              &turn_context.session_source,
 1311          ),
 1312      }
 1313  }

03
DIMENSION · MODELCONTEXT

模型与上下文

5 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factmodelContext

01 · 模型协议只保留 Responses API，但 Provider 端点与认证可扩展

源码事实

WireApi 枚举只有 Responses，反序列化 chat 会报迁移错误；ModelProviderInfo 允许自定义 base URL、环境密钥、命令认证、AWS SigV4、headers、query 和重试。

白话解释

它允许换“接线地址和门禁方式”，但要求对方都说 Responses 这门语言；不是任意 Chat Completions 方言翻译器。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼容面更一致，第三方 Provider 必须实现 Responses 语义而非只暴露 chat/completions。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 54–68
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 69–84
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   54  /// Wire protocol that the provider speaks.
   55  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Default, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, JsonSchema)]
   56  #[serde(rename_all = "lowercase")]
   57  pub enum WireApi {
   58      /// The Responses API exposed by OpenAI at `/v1/responses`.
   59      #[default]
   60      Responses,
   61  }
   62
   63  impl fmt::Display for WireApi {
   64      fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {
   65          let value = match self {
   66              Self::Responses => "responses",
   67          };
   68          f.write_str(value)
   69      }
   70  }
   71
   72  impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for WireApi {
   73      fn deserialize<D>(deserializer: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error>
   74      where
   75          D: serde::Deserializer<'de>,
   76      {
   77          let value = String::deserialize(deserializer)?;
   78          match value.as_str() {
   79              "responses" => Ok(Self::Responses),
   80              "chat" => Err(serde::de::Error::custom(CHAT_WIRE_API_REMOVED_ERROR)),
   81              _ => Err(serde::de::Error::unknown_variant(&value, &["responses"])),
   82          }
   83      }
   84  }

   87  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default, Deserialize, Serialize, PartialEq, JsonSchema)]
   88  #[schemars(deny_unknown_fields)]
   89  pub struct ModelProviderInfo {
   90      /// Friendly display name.
   91      #[serde(default)]
   92      pub name: String,
   93      /// Base URL for the provider's OpenAI-compatible API.
   94      pub base_url: Option<String>,
   95      /// Environment variable that stores the user's API key for this provider.
   96      pub env_key: Option<String>,
   97
   98      /// Optional instructions to help the user get a valid value for the
   99      /// variable and set it.
  100      pub env_key_instructions: Option<String>,
  101      /// Value to use with `Authorization: Bearer <token>` header. Use of this
  102      /// config is discouraged in favor of `env_key` for security reasons, but
  103      /// this may be necessary when using this programmatically.
  104      pub experimental_bearer_token: Option<String>,
  105      /// Command-backed bearer-token configuration for this provider.
  106      pub auth: Option<ModelProviderAuthInfo>,
  107      /// AWS SigV4 auth configuration for this provider.
  108      pub aws: Option<ModelProviderAwsAuthInfo>,
  109      /// Which wire protocol this provider expects.
  110      #[serde(default)]
  111      pub wire_api: WireApi,
  112      /// Optional query parameters to append to the base URL.
  113      pub query_params: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
  114      /// Additional HTTP headers to include in requests to this provider where
  115      /// the (key, value) pairs are the header name and value.
  116      pub http_headers: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
  117      /// Optional HTTP headers to include in requests to this provider where the
  118      /// (key, value) pairs are the header name and _environment variable_ whose
  119      /// value should be used. If the environment variable is not set, or the
  120      /// value is empty, the header will not be included in the request.
  121      pub env_http_headers: Option<HashMap<String, String>>,
  122      /// Maximum number of times to retry a failed HTTP request to this provider.
  123      pub request_max_retries: Option<u64>,
  124      /// Number of times to retry reconnecting a dropped streaming response before failing.
  125      pub stream_max_retries: Option<u64>,
  126      /// Idle timeout (in milliseconds) to wait for activity on a streaming response before treating
  127      /// the connection as lost.
  128      pub stream_idle_timeout_ms: Option<u64>,
  129      /// Maximum time (in milliseconds) to wait for a websocket connection attempt before treating
  130      /// it as failed.
  131      pub websocket_connect_timeout_ms: Option<u64>,
  132      /// Does this provider require an OpenAI API Key or ChatGPT login token? If true,
  133      /// user is presented with login screen on first run, and login preference and token/key
  134      /// are stored in auth.json. If false (which is the default), login screen is skipped,
  135      /// and API key (if needed) comes from the "env_key" environment variable.
  136      #[serde(default)]
  137      pub requires_openai_auth: bool,
  138      /// Whether this provider supports the Responses API WebSocket transport.
  139      #[serde(default)]
  140      pub supports_websockets: bool,
  141      /// Whether this provider supports the standalone web-search endpoint.
  142      #[serde(default)]
  143      pub supports_standalone_web_search: bool,
  144  }

  126      /// the connection as lost.
  127      pub stream_idle_timeout_ms: Option<u64>,
  128      /// Maximum time (in milliseconds) to wait for a websocket connection attempt before treating
  129      /// it as failed.
  130      pub websocket_connect_timeout_ms: Option<u64>,
  131      /// Does this provider require an OpenAI API Key or ChatGPT login token? If true,
  132      /// user is presented with login screen on first run, and login preference and token/key
  133      /// are stored in auth.json. If false (which is the default), login screen is skipped,
  134      /// and API key (if needed) comes from the "env_key" environment variable.
  135      #[serde(default)]
  136      pub requires_openai_auth: bool,
  137      /// Whether this provider supports the Responses API WebSocket transport.
  138      #[serde(default)]
  139      pub supports_websockets: bool,
  140      /// Whether this provider supports the standalone web-search endpoint.
  141      #[serde(default)]
  142      pub supports_standalone_web_search: bool,
  143  }
  144
  145  /// AWS SigV4 auth configuration for a model provider.
  146  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Deserialize, Serialize, PartialEq, Eq, JsonSchema)]
  147  #[schemars(deny_unknown_fields)]
  148  pub struct ModelProviderAwsAuthInfo {
  149      /// AWS profile name to use. When unset, the AWS SDK default chain decides.
  150      pub profile: Option<String>,
  151      /// AWS region to use for provider-specific endpoints.
  152      pub region: Option<String>,
  153  }

02
L1factmodelContext

02 · 传输层同时支持 SSE 与可复用 WebSocket，并带 turn 粘性状态

源码事实

ModelClientSession 按 turn 创建，延迟缓存 Responses WebSocket 和 x-codex-turn-state；预热是 generate=false 的 v2 response.create，失败后由常规重试/回退处理。

白话解释

每轮对话尽量占用一条可复用的高速通道，还带着本轮路由票据；热身失败不会把整轮任务判死。

对自研 Harness 的含义

交互延迟低，但 Provider 必须准确声明 WebSocket 能力，AWS 认证当前明确不能与 WebSocket 同开。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–11
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 12–24
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    1  //! Session- and turn-scoped helpers for talking to model provider APIs.
    2  //!
    3  //! `ModelClient` is intended to live for the lifetime of a Codex session and holds the stable
    4  //! configuration and state needed to talk to a provider (auth, provider selection, conversation id,
    5  //! and transport fallback state).
    6  //!
    7  //! Per-turn settings (model selection, reasoning controls, telemetry context, and turn metadata)
    8  //! are passed explicitly to streaming and unary methods so that the turn lifetime is visible at the
    9  //! call site.
   10  //!
   11  //! A [`ModelClientSession`] is created per turn and is used to stream one or more Responses API
   12  //! requests during that turn. It caches a Responses WebSocket connection (opened lazily) and stores
   13  //! per-turn state such as the `x-codex-turn-state` token used for sticky routing.
   14  //!
   15  //! WebSocket prewarm is a v2-only `response.create` with `generate=false`; it waits for completion
   16  //! so the next request can reuse the same connection and `previous_response_id`.
   17  //!
   18  //! Turn execution performs prewarm as a best-effort step before the first stream request so the
   19  //! subsequent request can reuse the same connection.
   20  //!
   21  //! ## Retry-Budget Tradeoff
   22  //!
   23  //! WebSocket prewarm is treated as the first websocket connection attempt for a turn. If it
   24  //! fails, normal stream retry/fallback logic handles recovery on the same turn.

03
L1factmodelContext

03 · 历史是带版本号的 Copy-on-Write 账本，不是随手拼接的消息数组

源码事实

ContextManager 用 Arc<Vec<ResponseItem>> 保存历史并维护 history_version、token info、reference context 和 world-state baseline；写入前过滤非 API item、截断并在取 prompt 时规范化。

白话解释

像有版本号的账本：读取者共享同一份快照，真要改时才复制，且每笔工具调用都要能对上回执。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合并发读取和回滚，也让 compaction、fork 与增量世界状态有明确一致性边界。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 38–48
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 49–60
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   38  /// Transcript of thread history
   39  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
   40  pub(crate) struct ContextManager {
   41      /// The oldest items are at the beginning of the vector. Snapshots share the vector until a
   42      /// caller needs to mutate it, avoiding deep copies for read-only history consumers.
   43      items: Arc<Vec<ResponseItem>>,
   44      /// Bumped whenever history is rewritten, such as compaction or rollback.
   45      history_version: u64,
   46      token_info: Option<TokenUsageInfo>,
   47      /// Reference context snapshot used for diffing and producing model-visible
   48      /// settings update items.
   49      ///
   50      /// This is the baseline for the next regular model turn, and may already
   51      /// match the current turn after context updates are persisted.
   52      ///
   53      /// When this is `None`, settings diffing treats the next turn as having no
   54      /// baseline and emits a full reinjection of context state. Rollback may
   55      /// also clear this when it trims a mixed initial-context developer bundle
   56      /// whose non-diff fragments no longer exist in the surviving history.
   57      reference_context_item: Option<TurnContextItem>,
   58      /// World state most recently appended to model-visible history.
   59      world_state_baseline: Option<WorldStateSnapshot>,
   60  }
   61
   62  impl ContextManager {
   63      pub(crate) fn new() -> Self {
   64          Self {
   65              items: Arc::new(Vec::new()),
   66              history_version: 0,
   67              token_info: TokenUsageInfo::new_or_append(
   68                  &None, &None, /*model_context_window*/ None,
   69              ),
   70              reference_context_item: None,
   71              world_state_baseline: None,
   72          }
   73      }
   74
   75      pub(crate) fn token_info(&self) -> Option<TokenUsageInfo> {
   76          self.token_info.clone()
   77      }
   78
   79      pub(crate) fn set_token_info(&mut self, info: Option<TokenUsageInfo>) {
   80          self.token_info = info;
   81      }
   82
   83      pub(crate) fn set_reference_context_item(&mut self, item: Option<TurnContextItem>) {
   84          self.reference_context_item = item;
   85      }
   86
   87      pub(crate) fn reference_context_item(&self) -> Option<TurnContextItem> {
   88          self.reference_context_item.clone()
   89      }
   90
   91      pub(crate) fn update_world_state(
   92          &mut self,
   93          world_state: &WorldState,
   94      ) -> (Vec<Box<dyn ContextualUserFragment>>, Option<WorldStateItem>) {
   95          let snapshot = world_state.snapshot();
   96          let fragments =
   97              world_state.render_history_diff(self.world_state_baseline.as_ref(), &self.items);
   98          let rollout_item = self.world_state_baseline.as_ref().map_or_else(
   99              || Some(WorldStateItem::full(snapshot.clone().into_value())),
  100              |previous| {
  101                  snapshot
  102                      .merge_patch_from(previous)
  103                      .map(WorldStateItem::patch)
  104              },
  105          );
  106          self.world_state_baseline = Some(snapshot);
  107          (fragments, rollout_item)
  108      }
  109
  110      pub(crate) fn set_world_state_baseline(&mut self, snapshot: WorldStateSnapshot) {
  111          self.world_state_baseline = Some(snapshot);
  112      }
  113
  114      pub(crate) fn set_token_usage_full(&mut self, context_window: i64) {
  115          match &mut self.token_info {
  116              Some(info) => info.fill_to_context_window(context_window),
  117              None => {
  118                  self.token_info = Some(TokenUsageInfo::full_context_window(context_window));
  119              }
  120          }
  121      }
  122
  123      /// `items` is ordered from oldest to newest.
  124      pub(crate) fn record_items<I>(&mut self, items: I, policy: TruncationPolicy)
  125      where
  126          I: IntoIterator,
  127          I::Item: std::ops::Deref<Target = ResponseItem>,
  128      {
  129          for item in items {
  130              let item_ref = item.deref();
  131              if !is_api_message(item_ref) {

   38  use std::ops::Deref;

   42  /// Transcript of thread history
   43  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
   44  pub(crate) struct ContextManager {
   45      /// The oldest items are at the beginning of the vector. Snapshots share the vector until a
   46      /// caller needs to mutate it, avoiding deep copies for read-only history consumers.
   47      items: Arc<Vec<ResponseItemEnvelope>>,
   48      /// Bumped whenever history is rewritten, such as compaction or rollback.
   49      history_version: u64,
   50      token_info: Option<TokenUsageInfo>,
   51      /// Reference context snapshot used for diffing and producing model-visible
   52      /// settings update items.
   53      ///
   54      /// This is the baseline for the next regular model turn, and may already
   55      /// match the current turn after context updates are persisted.
   56      ///
   57      /// When this is `None`, settings diffing treats the next turn as having no
   58      /// baseline and emits a full reinjection of context state. Rollback may
   59      /// also clear this when it trims a mixed initial-context developer bundle
   60      /// whose non-diff fragments no longer exist in the surviving history.
   61      reference_context_item: Option<TurnContextItem>,
   62      /// World state most recently appended to model-visible history.
   63      world_state_baseline: Option<WorldStateSnapshot>,
   64  }
   65

   68          Self {
   69              items: Arc::new(Vec::new()),
   70              history_version: 0,
   71              token_info: TokenUsageInfo::new_or_append(
   72                  &None, &None, /*model_context_window*/ None,
   73              ),
   74              reference_context_item: None,
   75              world_state_baseline: None,
   76          }
   77      }

   96          &mut self,
   97          world_state: &WorldState,
   98      ) -> (Vec<Box<dyn ContextualUserFragment>>, Option<WorldStateItem>) {
   99          let snapshot = world_state.snapshot();
  100          let fragments =
  101              world_state.render_history_diff(self.world_state_baseline.as_ref(), self.raw_items());
  102          let rollout_item = self.world_state_baseline.as_ref().map_or_else(
  103              || Some(WorldStateItem::full(snapshot.clone().into_object())),
  104              |previous| {
  105                  snapshot
  106                      .merge_patch_from(previous)
  107                      .map(WorldStateItem::patch)
  108              },
  109          );
  110          self.world_state_baseline = Some(snapshot);
  111          (fragments, rollout_item)
  112      }

  120              Some(info) => info.fill_to_context_window(context_window),
  121              None => {
  122                  self.token_info = Some(TokenUsageInfo::full_context_window(context_window));
  123              }
  124          }
  125      }

04
L1factmodelContext

04 · 上下文裁剪同时维护工具调用配对并计算多模态成本

源码事实

remove_oldest_item 会同步移除对应 call/output 并清 world baseline；normalize 修复配对并过滤不支持的图像/音频，token 估算覆盖 reasoning、compaction、图像、音频和 encrypted content。

白话解释

剪历史不能只撕掉一页：工具问题和答案必须一起处理；图片、音频和加密内容也不能当作零体积。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文预算比纯文本字符计数可靠，降低 Provider 因协议不完整而拒绝请求的概率。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 189–217
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 218–248
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  189      pub(crate) fn remove_first_item(&mut self) {
  190          if !self.items.is_empty() {
  191              // Remove the oldest item (front of the list). Items are ordered from
  192              // oldest → newest, so index 0 is the first entry recorded.
  193              let items = Arc::make_mut(&mut self.items);
  194              let removed = items.remove(0);
  195              // If the removed item participates in a call/output pair, also remove
  196              // its corresponding counterpart to keep the invariants intact without
  197              // running a full normalization pass.
  198              normalize::remove_corresponding_for(items, &removed);
  199              self.world_state_baseline = None;
  200          }
  201      }
  202
  203      pub(crate) fn replace(&mut self, items: Vec<ResponseItem>) {
  204          self.items = Arc::new(items);
  205          self.history_version = self.history_version.saturating_add(1);
  206          self.world_state_baseline = None;
  207      }
  208
  209      /// Drop the last `num_turns` instruction turns from this history.
  210      ///
  211      /// Instruction turns are history messages that should behave like a new prompt boundary:
  212      /// ordinary user messages and structured assistant inter-agent instructions.
  213      ///
  214      /// This mirrors thread-rollback semantics:
  215      /// - `num_turns == 0` is a no-op
  216      /// - if there are no user turns, this is a no-op
  217      /// - if `num_turns` exceeds the number of user turns, all user turns are dropped while
  218      ///   preserving any items that occurred before the first user message.
  219      ///
  220      /// If rollback trims a pre-turn developer message that mixes contextual fragments with
  221      /// persistent developer text from `build_initial_context`, this also clears
  222      /// `reference_context_item`. The surviving history no longer contains the full bundle that
  223      /// established the prior baseline, so future turns must fall back to full reinjection instead
  224      /// of diffing against stale state.
  225      pub(crate) fn drop_last_n_user_turns(&mut self, num_turns: u32) {
  226          if num_turns == 0 {
  227              return;
  228          }
  229
  230          let snapshot = self.items.clone();
  231          let user_positions = user_message_positions(&snapshot);
  232          let Some(&first_instruction_turn_idx) = user_positions.first() else {
  233              self.replace(Arc::unwrap_or_clone(snapshot));
  234              return;
  235          };
  236
  237          let n_from_end = usize::try_from(num_turns).unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
  238          let mut cut_idx = if n_from_end >= user_positions.len() {
  239              first_instruction_turn_idx
  240          } else {
  241              user_positions[user_positions.len() - n_from_end]
  242          };
  243
  244          cut_idx =
  245              self.trim_pre_turn_context_updates(&snapshot, first_instruction_turn_idx, cut_idx);
  246
  247          self.replace(snapshot[..cut_idx].to_vec());
  248      }
  249
  250      pub(crate) fn update_token_info(
  251          &mut self,
  252          usage: &TokenUsage,
  253          model_context_window: Option<i64>,
  254      ) {
  255          self.token_info = TokenUsageInfo::new_or_append(
  256              &self.token_info,
  257              &Some(usage.clone()),
  258              model_context_window,
  259          );
  260      }
  261
  262      fn get_non_last_reasoning_items_tokens(&self) -> i64 {
  263          // Get reasoning items excluding all the ones after the last instruction boundary.
  264          let Some(last_user_index) = self.items.iter().rposition(is_user_turn_boundary) else {
  265              return 0;
  266          };
  267
  268          self.items
  269              .iter()
  270              .take(last_user_index)
  271              .filter(|item| {
  272                  matches!(
  273                      item,
  274                      ResponseItem::Reasoning {
  275                          encrypted_content: Some(_),
  276                          ..
  277                      }
  278                  )
  279              })
  280              .map(estimate_item_token_count)
  281              .fold(0i64, i64::saturating_add)
  282      }
  283
  284      // These are local items added after the most recent model-emitted item.
  285      // They are not reflected in `last_token_usage.total_tokens`.
  286      fn items_after_last_model_generated_item(&self) -> &[ResponseItem] {
  287          let start = self
  288              .items
  289              .iter()
  290              .rposition(is_model_generated_item)
  291              .map_or(self.items.len(), |index| index.saturating_add(1));
  292          &self.items[start..]
  293      }
  294
  295      /// When true, the server already accounted for past reasoning tokens and
  296      /// the client should not re-estimate them.
  297      pub(crate) fn get_total_token_usage(&self, server_reasoning_included: bool) -> i64 {
  298          let last_tokens = self
  299              .token_info
  300              .as_ref()
  301              .map(|info| info.last_token_usage.total_tokens)
  302              .unwrap_or(0);
  303          let items_after_last_model_generated_tokens = self
  304              .items_after_last_model_generated_item()
  305              .iter()
  306              .map(estimate_item_token_count)
  307              .fold(0i64, i64::saturating_add);
  308          if server_reasoning_included {
  309              last_tokens.saturating_add(items_after_last_model_generated_tokens)
  310          } else {
  311              last_tokens
  312                  .saturating_add(self.get_non_last_reasoning_items_tokens())
  313                  .saturating_add(items_after_last_model_generated_tokens)
  314          }
  315      }
  316
  317      pub(crate) fn estimated_tokens_after_last_model_generated_item(&self) -> i64 {
  318          self.items_after_last_model_generated_item()
  319              .iter()

  223              text: model_info.get_model_instructions(personality),

  229      pub(crate) fn estimate_token_count_with_base_instructions(
  230          &self,
  231          base_instructions: &BaseInstructions,
  232      ) -> Option<i64> {
  233          let base_tokens =
  234              i64::try_from(approx_token_count(&base_instructions.text)).unwrap_or(i64::MAX);
  235
  236          let items_tokens = self
  237              .items
  238              .iter()
  239              .map(|envelope| estimate_item_token_count(&envelope.item))
  240              .fold(0i64, i64::saturating_add);
  241
  242          Some(base_tokens.saturating_add(items_tokens))
  243      }

05
L1factmodelContext

05 · 本地压缩会自救：压缩请求自身超限时逐项删旧记录再重试

源码事实

compact 使用模型生成摘要；若压缩调用本身超出窗口，就删除最老 item 及其配对继续尝试，成功后保留真实 user 消息、插入摘要前缀、推进窗口并重算 usage。

白话解释

连“请帮我整理行李”这句话都塞不进去时，它会先扔掉最旧且成对的票据，直到能完成整理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

长会话更耐用；源码也明确提示多次摘要会逐步降低准确性。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 240–278
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 279–318
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/compact.rs:240–318固定提交 ↗
  240  async fn run_compact_task_inner_impl(
  241      sess: Arc<Session>,
  242      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  243      input: Vec<UserInput>,
  244      initial_context_injection: InitialContextInjection,
  245      compaction_metadata: CompactionTurnMetadata,
  246  ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  247      let compaction_item = TurnItem::ContextCompaction(ContextCompactionItem::new());
  248      sess.emit_turn_item_started(&turn_context, &compaction_item)
  249          .await;
  250      let initial_input_for_turn: ResponseInputItem = ResponseInputItem::from(input);
  251
  252      let mut history = sess.clone_history().await;
  253      history.record_items(
  254          &[initial_input_for_turn.into()],
  255          turn_context.model_info.truncation_policy.into(),
  256      );
  257
  258      let max_retries = turn_context.provider.info().stream_max_retries();
  259      let mut retries = 0;
  260      let mut client_session = sess.services.model_client.new_session();
  261      // Reuse one client session so turn-scoped state (sticky routing, websocket incremental
  262      // request tracking)
  263      // survives retries within this compact turn.
  264      let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  265      let responses_metadata = turn_context.turn_metadata_state.to_responses_metadata(
  266          sess.installation_id.clone(),
  267          window_id,
  268          CodexResponsesRequestKind::Compaction(compaction_metadata),
  269      );
  270
  271      loop {
  272          // Clone is required because of the loop
  273          let turn_input = history
  274              .clone()
  275              .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities);
  276          let turn_input_len = turn_input.len();
  277          let prompt = Prompt {
  278              input: turn_input,
  279              base_instructions: sess.get_base_instructions().await,
  280              ..Default::default()
  281          };
  282          let attempt_result = drain_to_completed(
  283              &sess,
  284              turn_context.as_ref(),
  285              &mut client_session,
  286              &responses_metadata,
  287              &prompt,
  288          )
  289          .await;
  290
  291          match attempt_result {
  292              Ok(()) => {
  293                  break;
  294              }
  295              Err(err)
  296                  if matches!(
  297                      err.details(),
  298                      CodexErrorDetails::Interrupted | CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted
  299                  ) =>
  300              {
  301                  return Err(err);
  302              }
  303              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::SessionBudgetExceeded) => {
  304                  sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  305                  let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  306                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  307                  return Err(e);
  308              }
  309              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::ContextWindowExceeded) => {
  310                  if turn_input_len > 1 {
  311                      // Trim from the beginning to preserve cache (prefix-based) and keep recent messages intact.
  312                      error!(
  313                          "Context window exceeded while compacting; removing oldest history item. Error: {e}"
  314                      );
  315                      history.remove_first_item();
  316                      retries = 0;
  317                      continue;
  318                  }
  319                  sess.set_total_tokens_full(turn_context.as_ref()).await;
  320                  sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  321                  let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  322                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  323                  return Err(e);
  324              }
  325              Err(e) => {
  326                  if retries < max_retries {
  327                      retries += 1;
  328                      let delay = backoff(retries);
  329                      sess.notify_stream_error(
  330                          turn_context.as_ref(),
  331                          format!("Reconnecting... {retries}/{max_retries}"),
  332                          e,
  333                      )
  334                      .await;
  335                      tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
  336                      continue;
  337                  } else {
  338                      sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  339                      let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  340                      sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  341                      return Err(e);
  342                  }
  343              }
  344          }
  345      }
  346
  347      let history_snapshot = sess.clone_history().await;
  348      let history_items = history_snapshot.raw_items();
  349      let summary_suffix = get_last_assistant_message_from_turn(history_items).unwrap_or_default();
  350      let summary_text = format!("{SUMMARY_PREFIX}\n{summary_suffix}");
  351      let user_messages = collect_user_messages(history_items);
  352
  353      let mut new_history = build_compacted_history(Vec::new(), &user_messages, &summary_text);
  354      if let Some(summary_item) = new_history.last_mut() {
  355          // This replacement history skips `record_conversation_items`; only the appended summary
  356          // belongs to this compaction turn.
  357          summary_item.set_turn_id_if_missing(&turn_context.sub_id);
  358      }
  359      let (window_number, window_ids) = sess.advance_auto_compact_window().await;
  360
  361      let (initial_context, world_state_baseline) =
  362          build_compaction_initial_context(sess.as_ref(), &initial_context_injection).await;
  363      if !initial_context.is_empty() {
  364          new_history =
  365              insert_initial_context_before_last_real_user_or_summary(new_history, initial_context);
  366      }
  367      let reference_context_item = match initial_context_injection {
  368          InitialContextInjection::DoNotInject => None,
  369          InitialContextInjection::BeforeLastUserMessage { .. } => {
  370              Some(turn_context.to_turn_context_item())
  371          }
  372      };
  373      sess.replace_compacted_history(
  374          new_history,
  375          reference_context_item,
  376          world_state_baseline,
  377          CompactedHistoryMetadata {
  378              message: summary_text,
  379              window_number,
  380              window_ids,
  381          },
  382      )
  383      .await;
  384      sess.recompute_token_usage(&turn_context).await;
  385
  386      sess.emit_turn_item_completed(&turn_context, compaction_item)
  387          .await;
  388      let warning = EventMsg::Warning(WarningEvent {
  389          message: "Heads up: Long threads and multiple compactions can cause the model to be less accurate. Start a new thread when possible to keep threads small and targeted.".to_string(),
展开 28 行逐行翻译

  240  async fn run_compact_task_inner_impl(
  241      sess: Arc<Session>,
  242      turn_context: Arc<TurnContext>,
  243      input: Vec<UserInput>,
  244      initial_context_injection: InitialContextInjection,
  245      compaction_metadata: CompactionTurnMetadata,
  246  ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  247      let compaction_item = TurnItem::ContextCompaction(ContextCompactionItem::new());
  248      sess.emit_turn_item_started(&turn_context, &compaction_item)
  249          .await;
  250      let initial_input_for_turn: ResponseInputItem = ResponseInputItem::from(input);
  251
  252      let mut history = sess.clone_history().await;
  253      history.record_items(
  254          &[initial_input_for_turn.into()],
  255          turn_context.model_info.truncation_policy.into(),
  256      );
  257
  258      let max_retries = turn_context.provider.info().stream_max_retries();
  259      let mut retries = 0;
  260      let mut client_session = sess.services.model_client.new_session();
  261      // Reuse one client session so turn-scoped state (sticky routing, websocket incremental
  262      // request tracking)
  263      // survives retries within this compact turn.
  264      let window_id = sess.current_window_id().await;
  265      let responses_metadata = turn_context.turn_metadata_state.to_responses_metadata(
  266          sess.installation_id.clone(),
  267          window_id,
  268          CodexResponsesRequestKind::Compaction(compaction_metadata),
  269      );
  270
  271      loop {
  272          // Clone is required because of the loop
  273          let turn_input = history
  274              .clone()
  275              .for_prompt(&turn_context.model_info.input_modalities);
  276          let turn_input_len = turn_input.len();
  277          let prompt = Prompt {
  278              input: turn_input,
  279              base_instructions: sess.get_base_instructions().await,
  280              ..Default::default()
  281          };
  282          let attempt_result = drain_to_completed(
  283              &sess,
  284              turn_context.as_ref(),
  285              &mut client_session,
  286              &responses_metadata,
  287              &prompt,
  288          )
  289          .await;
  290

  292              Ok(()) => {
  293                  break;
  294              }
  295              Err(err)
  296                  if matches!(
  297                      err.details(),
  298                      CodexErrorDetails::Interrupted | CodexErrorDetails::TurnAborted
  299                  ) =>
  300              {
  301                  return Err(err);
  302              }
  303              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::SessionBudgetExceeded) => {
  304                  sess.track_turn_codex_error(turn_context.as_ref(), &e);
  305                  let event = EventMsg::Error(e.to_error_event(/*message_prefix*/ None));
  306                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  307                  return Err(e);
  308              }
  309              Err(e) if matches!(e.details(), CodexErrorDetails::ContextWindowExceeded) => {

  312                      error!(

  314                      );

  318                  }

  322                  sess.send_event(&turn_context, event).await;
  323                  return Err(e);
  324              }
  325              Err(e) => {

  337                  } else {

  356          // This replacement history skips `record_conversation_items`; only the appended summary
  357          // belongs to this compaction turn.

DIMENSION · TOOLS

工具注册与执行

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1facttools

01 · 工具有统一 typed runtime 契约，hooks、观测和流式参数 diff 都是一级能力

源码事实

CoreToolRuntime 扩展 ToolExecutor，统一声明 payload 匹配、取消清理、telemetry tags、pre/post hook payload、hook 输入改写和参数 diff consumer。

白话解释

每个新工具不只是写一个 execute 函数，还要说明如何取消、怎么记日志、钩子看什么、参数流到一半时如何展示。

对自研 Harness 的含义

扩展成本高于轻量 Agent，但跨工具的安全和观测语义更一致。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 48–97
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 98–149
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   48  /// Typed runtime contract for locally executed tools.
   49  ///
   50  /// Implementers provide the shared `ToolExecutor` behavior plus optional
   51  /// core-owned metadata for hooks, telemetry, tool search, and argument diffs.
   52  pub(crate) trait CoreToolRuntime: ToolExecutor<ToolInvocation> {
   53      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
   54          matches!(
   55              payload,
   56              ToolPayload::Function { .. } | ToolPayload::ToolSearch { .. }
   57          )
   58      }
   59
   60      /// Whether cancellation should let the handler finish teardown before the
   61      /// host returns an aborted tool response.
   62      fn waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&self) -> bool {
   63          false
   64      }
   65
   66      fn telemetry_tags<'a>(
   67          &'a self,
   68          _invocation: &'a ToolInvocation,
   69      ) -> BoxFuture<'a, ToolTelemetryTags> {
   70          Box::pin(async { Vec::new() })
   71      }
   72
   73      fn post_tool_use_payload(
   74          &self,
   75          invocation: &ToolInvocation,
   76          result: &dyn ToolOutput,
   77      ) -> Option<PostToolUsePayload> {
   78          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
   79              return None;
   80          };
   81
   82          Some(PostToolUsePayload {
   83              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
   84              tool_use_id: result.post_tool_use_id(&invocation.call_id),
   85              tool_input: result
   86                  .post_tool_use_input(&invocation.payload)
   87                  .unwrap_or_else(|| function_hook_tool_input(arguments)),
   88              tool_response: result
   89                  .post_tool_use_response(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
   90                  .or_else(|| {
   91                      // Most function tools can expose their model-facing output
   92                      // as the hook response. Outputs with a more stable hook
   93                      // contract should override post_tool_use_response above.
   94                      let ResponseInputItem::FunctionCallOutput {
   95                          output: FunctionCallOutputPayload { body, .. },
   96                          ..
   97                      } = result.to_response_item(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
   98                      else {
   99                          return None;
  100                      };
  101
  102                      serde_json::to_value(body).ok()
  103                  })?,
  104          })
  105      }
  106
  107      fn pre_tool_use_payload(&self, invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> Option<PreToolUsePayload> {
  108          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
  109              return None;
  110          };
  111
  112          Some(PreToolUsePayload {
  113              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  114              tool_input: function_hook_tool_input(arguments),
  115          })
  116      }
  117
  118      /// Rebuilds a tool invocation from hook-facing `tool_input`.
  119      ///
  120      /// Tools that opt into input-rewriting hooks should invert the same stable
  121      /// hook contract they expose from `pre_tool_use_payload`.
  122      fn with_updated_hook_input(
  123          &self,
  124          invocation: ToolInvocation,
  125          updated_input: Value,
  126      ) -> Result<ToolInvocation, FunctionCallError> {
  127          let ToolPayload::Function { .. } = &invocation.payload else {
  128              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  129                  "hook input rewrite received unsupported function tool payload".to_string(),
  130              ));
  131          };
  132
  133          let arguments = serde_json::to_string(&updated_input).map_err(|err| {
  134              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(format!(
  135                  "failed to serialize rewritten {} arguments: {err}",
  136                  flat_tool_name(&invocation.tool_name)
  137              ))
  138          })?;
  139          Ok(ToolInvocation {
  140              payload: ToolPayload::Function { arguments },
  141              ..invocation
  142          })
  143      }
  144
  145      /// Creates an optional consumer for streamed tool argument diffs.
  146      fn create_diff_consumer(&self) -> Option<Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>> {
  147          None
  148      }
  149  }
  150
  151  /// Consumes streamed argument diffs for a tool call and emits protocol events
  152  /// derived from partial tool input.
  153  pub(crate) trait ToolArgumentDiffConsumer: Send {
  154      /// Consume the next argument diff for a tool call.
  155      fn consume_diff(&mut self, turn: &TurnContext, call_id: String, diff: &str)
  156      -> Option<EventMsg>;
  157
  158      /// Finish consuming argument diffs before the tool call completes.
  159      fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Option<EventMsg>, FunctionCallError> {
  160          Ok(None)
  161      }
  162  }

  166      pub(crate) payload: ToolPayload,
  167      pub(crate) result: Box<dyn ToolOutput>,
  168      pub(crate) post_tool_use_payload: Option<PostToolUsePayload>,

  174              call_id,
  175              payload,
  176              result,
  177              ..
  178          } = self;
  179          result.to_response_item(&call_id, &payload)

  183          let Self {
  184              payload, result, ..

   49  /// Typed runtime contract for locally executed tools.
   50  ///
   51  /// Implementers provide the shared `ToolExecutor` behavior plus optional
   52  /// core-owned metadata for hooks, telemetry, tool search, and argument diffs.
   53  pub(crate) trait CoreToolRuntime: ToolExecutor<ToolInvocation> {
   54      /// Returns a shared spec when both the spec and search metadata are immutable.
   55      fn immutable_spec(&self) -> Option<&Arc<ToolSpec>> {
   56          None
   57      }
   58
   59      /// Returns lazily cached Code Mode definitions owned by this runtime.
   60      fn cached_code_mode_definitions(&self) -> Option<&[codex_code_mode::ToolDefinition]> {
   61          None
   62      }
   63
   64      /// Returns a readiness wait for this exact tool before taking the execution gate.
   65      fn wait_until_ready<'a>(&'a self, _session: &'a Arc<Session>) -> Option<BoxFuture<'a, ()>> {
   66          None
   67      }
   68
   69      /// Returns the owning server only for MCP-backed tool runtimes.
   70      fn mcp_server_name(&self) -> Option<&str> {
   71          None
   72      }
   73
   74      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
   75          matches!(
   76              payload,
   77              ToolPayload::Function { .. } | ToolPayload::ToolSearch { .. }
   78          )
   79      }
   80
   81      /// Whether cancellation should let the handler finish teardown before the
   82      /// host returns an aborted tool response.
   83      fn waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&self) -> bool {
   84          false
   85      }
   86
   87      fn telemetry_tags(&self, _invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> ToolTelemetryTags {
   88          Vec::new()
   89      }
   90
   91      fn post_tool_use_payload(
   92          &self,
   93          invocation: &ToolInvocation,
   94          result: &dyn ToolOutput,
   95      ) -> Option<PostToolUsePayload> {
   96          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
   97              return None;
   98          };
   99
  100          Some(PostToolUsePayload {
  101              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  102              tool_use_id: result.post_tool_use_id(&invocation.call_id),
  103              tool_input: result
  104                  .post_tool_use_input(&invocation.payload)
  105                  .unwrap_or_else(|| function_hook_tool_input(arguments)),
  106              tool_response: result
  107                  .post_tool_use_response(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
  108                  .or_else(|| {
  109                      // Most function tools can expose their model-facing output
  110                      // as the hook response. Outputs with a more stable hook
  111                      // contract should override post_tool_use_response above.
  112                      let ResponseInputItem::FunctionCallOutput {
  113                          output: FunctionCallOutputPayload { body, .. },
  114                          ..
  115                      } = result.to_response_item(&invocation.call_id, &invocation.payload)
  116                      else {
  117                          return None;
  118                      };
  119
  120                      serde_json::to_value(body).ok()
  121                  })?,
  122          })
  123      }
  124
  125      fn pre_tool_use_payload(&self, invocation: &ToolInvocation) -> Option<PreToolUsePayload> {
  126          let ToolPayload::Function { arguments } = &invocation.payload else {
  127              return None;
  128          };
  129
  130          Some(PreToolUsePayload {
  131              tool_name: function_hook_tool_name(invocation),
  132              tool_input: function_hook_tool_input(arguments),
  133          })
  134      }
  135
  136      /// Rebuilds a tool invocation from hook-facing `tool_input`.
  137      ///
  138      /// Tools that opt into input-rewriting hooks should invert the same stable
  139      /// hook contract they expose from `pre_tool_use_payload`.
  140      fn with_updated_hook_input(
  141          &self,
  142          invocation: ToolInvocation,
  143          updated_input: Value,
  144      ) -> Result<ToolInvocation, FunctionCallError> {
  145          let ToolPayload::Function { .. } = &invocation.payload else {
  146              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  147                  "hook input rewrite received unsupported function tool payload".to_string(),
  148              ));
  149          };
  150
  151          let arguments = serde_json::to_string(&updated_input).map_err(|err| {
  152              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(format!(
  153                  "failed to serialize rewritten {} arguments: {err}",
  154                  flat_tool_name(&invocation.tool_name)
  155              ))
  156          })?;
  157          Ok(ToolInvocation {
  158              payload: ToolPayload::Function { arguments },
  159              ..invocation
  160          })
  161      }
  162
  163      /// Creates an optional consumer for streamed tool argument diffs.
  164      fn create_diff_consumer(&self) -> Option<Box<dyn ToolArgumentDiffConsumer>> {
  165          None
  166      }
  167  }
  168
  169  /// Consumes streamed argument diffs for a tool call and emits protocol events
  170  /// derived from partial tool input.
  171  pub(crate) trait ToolArgumentDiffConsumer: Send {
  172      /// Consume the next argument diff for a tool call.
  173      fn consume_diff(&mut self, turn: &TurnContext, call_id: String, diff: &str)
  174      -> Option<EventMsg>;
  175
  176      /// Finish consuming argument diffs before the tool call completes.
  177      fn finish(&mut self) -> Result<Option<EventMsg>, FunctionCallError> {
  178          Ok(None)
  179      }
  180  }
  181
  182  pub(crate) struct AnyToolResult {
  183      pub(crate) call_id: String,
  184      pub(crate) payload: ToolPayload,
  185      pub(crate) result: Box<dyn ToolOutput>,
  186      pub(crate) post_tool_use_payload: Option<PostToolUsePayload>,
  187  }

  189  impl AnyToolResult {
  190      pub(crate) fn into_response(self) -> ResponseInputItem {
  191          let Self {
  192              call_id,
  193              payload,
  194              result,
  195              ..
  196          } = self;
  197          result.to_response_item(&call_id, &payload)
  198      }
  199
  200      pub(crate) fn code_mode_result(self) -> serde_json::Value {
  201          let Self {
  202              payload, result, ..
  203          } = self;
  204          result.code_mode_result(&payload)
  205      }
  206  }

  208  struct PostToolUseFeedbackOutput {
  209      original: Box<dyn ToolOutput>,
  210      model_visible: FunctionToolOutput,
  211  }
  212
  213  impl ToolOutput for PostToolUseFeedbackOutput {
  214      fn log_preview(&self) -> String {
  215          self.original.log_preview()
  216      }
  217
  218      fn success_for_logging(&self) -> bool {
  219          self.original.success_for_logging()
  220      }
02
L1facttools

02 · 并发工具用读写锁实现“并行组 + 全局排他工具”

源码事实

ToolCallRuntime 对支持并发的 handler 取读锁，不支持并发的取写锁；取消时按 handler 语义等待清理或 abort，并输出结构化 aborted result 与分段时延。

白话解释

能并行的工具像多人同时读资料；危险或有状态的工具拿独占钥匙，等所有并行动作结束后自己运行。

对自研 Harness 的含义

不是简单 Promise.all，避免非并行工具与其他调用交叉污染环境。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 74–108
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 109–145
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   74      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
   75      pub(crate) fn handle_tool_call(

   80          let error_call = call.clone();
   81          let future =
   82              self.handle_tool_call_with_source(call, ToolCallSource::Direct, cancellation_token);
   83          async move {
   84              match future.await {
   85                  Ok(response) => Ok(response.into_response()),
   86                  Err(FunctionCallError::Fatal(message)) => Err(CodexErr::Fatal(message)),
   87                  Err(other) => Ok(Self::failure_response(error_call, other)),
   88              }

   92
   93      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
   94      pub(crate) fn handle_tool_call_with_source(
   95          self,
   96          call: ToolCall,
   97          source: ToolCallSource,
   98          cancellation_token: CancellationToken,
   99      ) -> impl std::future::Future<Output = Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> {
  100          let supports_parallel = self.router.tool_supports_parallel(&call);
  101          let router = Arc::clone(&self.router);
  102          let session = Arc::clone(&self.session);
  103          let step_context = Arc::clone(&self.step_context);
  104          let turn = Arc::clone(&step_context.turn);
  105          let tracker = Arc::clone(&self.tracker);
  106          let lock = Arc::clone(&self.parallel_execution);
  107          let invocation_cancellation_token = cancellation_token.clone();
  108          let wait_for_runtime_cancellation = self.router.tool_waits_for_runtime_cancellation(&call);
  109          let started = Instant::now();
  110          let tool_call_timing_guard =
  111              ToolCallTimingGuard::capture(started, &session.thread_id, &turn.sub_id, &call, &source);
  112          let execution_started_at = tool_call_timing_guard
  113              .as_ref()
  114              .map(|timing| Arc::clone(&timing.execution_started_at));
  115          let abort_session = Arc::clone(&session);
  116          let abort_source = source.clone();
  117          let abort_turn = Arc::clone(&turn);
  118          let terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));
  119          let dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached = Arc::clone(&terminal_outcome_reached);
  120          let dispatch_call = call.clone();
  121
  122          let dispatch_span = trace_span!(
  123              "dispatch_tool_call_with_code_mode_result",
  124              otel.name = %call.tool_name,
  125              tool_name = %call.tool_name,
  126              call_id = call.call_id.as_str(),
  127              aborted = false,
  128          );
  129          let abort_dispatch_span = dispatch_span.clone();
  130
  131          let mut dispatch_handle: AbortOnDropHandle<Result<AnyToolResult, FunctionCallError>> =
  132              AbortOnDropHandle::new(tokio::spawn(async move {
  133                  let _guard = if supports_parallel {
  134                      Either::Left(lock.read().await)
  135                  } else {
  136                      Either::Right(lock.write().await)
  137                  };
  138                  // Admission through the parallel-execution gate marks the end
  139                  // of dispatch waiting and the start of handler execution.
  140                  if let Some(execution_started_at) = execution_started_at {
  141                      let _ = execution_started_at.set(Instant::now());
  142                  }
  143
  144                  router
  145                      .dispatch_tool_call_with_terminal_outcome(
  146                          session,
  147                          step_context,
  148                          invocation_cancellation_token,
  149                          tracker,
  150                          dispatch_call,
  151                          source,
  152                          dispatch_terminal_outcome_reached,
  153                      )
  154                      .instrument(dispatch_span.clone())
  155                      .await
  156              }));
  157
  158          async move {
  159              let _tool_call_timing_guard = tool_call_timing_guard;
  160              tokio::select! {
  161                  res = &mut dispatch_handle => res.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?,
  162                  _ = cancellation_token.cancelled() => {
  163                      if terminal_outcome_reached.load(Ordering::Acquire) || dispatch_handle.is_finished() {
  164                          dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?
  165                      } else {
  166                          let secs = started.elapsed().as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
  167                          abort_dispatch_span.record("aborted", true);
  168                          if wait_for_runtime_cancellation {
  169                              if terminal_outcome_reached.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
  170                                  return dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?;
  171                              }
  172                              // The abort owns the terminal outcome; await only so
  173                              // the runtime can finish process teardown.
  174                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  175                                  Ok(_) => {}
  176                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  177                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  178                              }
  179                          } else {
  180                              dispatch_handle.abort();
  181                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  182                                  Ok(result) => return result,
  183                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  184                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  185                              }
  186                          }
  187                          let response = Self::aborted_response(&call, secs);
  188                          notify_tool_aborted(
  189                              abort_session.as_ref(),
  190                              abort_turn.as_ref(),
  191                              call.call_id.as_str(),
  192                              &call.tool_name,
  193                              abort_source,
  194                          )
  195                          .await;
  196                          Ok(response)
  197                      }
  198                  },
  199              }
  200          }
  201          .in_current_span()
  202      }
  203  }
  204
