  181                  res = &mut dispatch_handle => res.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?,
  182                  _ = cancellation_token.cancelled() => {
  183                      if terminal_outcome_reached.load(Ordering::Acquire) || dispatch_handle.is_finished() {
  184                          dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?
  185                      } else {
  186                          let secs = started.elapsed().as_secs_f32().max(0.1);
  187                          abort_dispatch_span.record("aborted", true);
  188                          if wait_for_runtime_cancellation {
  189                              if terminal_outcome_reached.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
  190                                  return dispatch_handle.await.map_err(Self::tool_task_join_error)?;
  191                              }
  192                              // The abort owns the terminal outcome; await only so
  193                              // the runtime can finish process teardown.
  194                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  195                                  Ok(_) => {}
  196                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  197                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  198                              }
  199                          } else {
  200                              dispatch_handle.abort();
  201                              match dispatch_handle.await {
  202                                  Ok(result) => return result,
  203                                  Err(err) if err.is_cancelled() => {}
  204                                  Err(err) => return Err(Self::tool_task_join_error(err)),
  205                              }
  206                          }
  207                          let response = Self::aborted_response(&call, secs);
  208                          notify_tool_aborted(
  209                              abort_session.as_ref(),
  210                              abort_turn.as_ref(),
  211                              call.call_id.as_str(),
  212                              &call.tool_name,
  213                              abort_source,
  214                          )
  215                          .await;
  216                          Ok(response)
03
L3facttools

03 · 工具暴露与分发分离，兼容旧 shell 又不污染模型可见清单

源码事实

工具规划测试确认 unified exec 可把 exec_command/write_stdin 暴露给模型，同时保留隐藏的 legacy shell_command 仅供分发；apply_patch、web search 和动态工具也按环境、模型及 Provider capability gate。

白话解释

后台可以保留旧插座做兼容，但模型眼前只摆当前应该用的工具，避免同功能多把扳手。

对自研 Harness 的含义

迁移期兼容性强，且 prompt tool schema 保持紧凑稳定。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 637–671
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 672–708
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

06
DIMENSION · CONNECTORS

连接器、插件与 MCP

2 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factconnectors

01 · MCP 是带复用、认证、required gate 和 catalog revision 的运行时

源码事实

McpConnectionSet 同时管理 server、required servers、工具目录版本、Codex Apps cache、plugin provenance、工具命名前缀和 elicitation；连接配置/OAuth 凭据一致且存活时才复用。

白话解释

它不是每轮临时扫一遍外接工具，而是维护一套有版本、有健康状态、有认证身份的连接池。

对自研 Harness 的含义

工具清单可稳定捕获到 step snapshot，连接变化不会悄悄污染正在执行的一步。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/codex-mcp/src/connection_manager.rs:66–117固定提交 ↗
   66  pub(crate) struct McpServerConnection {
   67      identity: Option<McpServerConnectionIdentity>,
   68      client: AsyncManagedClient,
   69  }
   70
   71  impl McpServerConnection {
   72      async fn reusable_client(
   73          &self,
   74          desired: &McpServerConnectionIdentity,
   75      ) -> Option<ManagedClient> {
   76          let current = self.identity.as_ref()?;
   77          if !current.has_same_connection_config(desired) {
   78              return None;
   79          }
   80          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
   81              return None;
   82          }
   83          let client = self.client.client().await.ok()?;
   84          if client.client.is_closed().await {
   85              return None;
   86          }
   87          let Ok(desired_credentials) = desired.oauth_credentials() else {
   88              return Some(client);
   89          };
   90          let reusable = match client.client.managed_oauth_credentials().await {
   91              Some(live_credentials) => &live_credentials == desired_credentials,
   92              None => current
   93                  .oauth_credentials()
   94                  .is_ok_and(|startup_credentials| startup_credentials == desired_credentials),
   95          };
   96          if reusable { Some(client) } else { None }
   97      }
   98
   99      pub(crate) async fn client(&self) -> Result<ManagedClient, StartupOutcomeError> {
  100          self.client.client().await
  101      }
  102
  103      async fn shutdown(&self) {
  104          self.client.shutdown().await;
  105      }
  106
  107      fn cancel_startup(&self) {
  108          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
  109              self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  110          }
  111      }
  112  }
  113
  114  impl Drop for McpServerConnection {
  115      fn drop(&mut self) {
  116          self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  117      }
  118  }
  119
  120  #[derive(Clone)]
  121  struct McpServerView {
  122      connection: Arc<McpServerConnection>,
  123      metadata: McpServerMetadata,
  124      tool_filter: ToolFilter,
  125      tool_timeout: Option<Duration>,
  126  }
  127
  128  impl McpServerView {
  129      async fn listed_tools(
  130          &self,
  131          tool_plugin_provenance: &ToolPluginProvenance,
  132      ) -> Option<Vec<ToolInfo>> {
  133          let tools = self.connection.client.listed_tools().await?;
  134          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &self.tool_filter);
  135          Some(if self.connection.client.is_codex_apps_mcp_server {
  136              prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  137          } else {
  138              prepare_regular_mcp_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  139          })
  140      }
  141  }
  142
  143  /// A published view over a set of running MCP server connections.

  149      codex_apps_refresh_lock: Mutex<()>,

  153      elicitation_requests: ElicitationRequestManager,
  154  }

  157      /// Creates an MCP connection manager. Threadless callers can pass no `tx_event`; startup
  158      /// notifications are then skipped and interactive elicitations are declined.

  182          } = input;

  184          let keyring_backend_kind = config.auth_keyring_backend_kind;
  185          let approval_policy = &config.approval_policy;
  186          let initial_permission_profile = config.permission_profile.clone();
  187          let codex_home = config.codex_home.clone();
  188          let prefix_mcp_tool_names = config.prefix_mcp_tool_names;

   66  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupFailure;
   67  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupFailureReason;
   68  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupStatus;
   69  use codex_protocol::protocol::McpStartupUpdateEvent;
   70  use codex_rmcp_client::determine_streamable_http_auth_status_from_credentials;
   71  use tokio::sync::Mutex;
   72  use tokio::sync::RwLock;
   73  use tokio::sync::watch;
   74  use tokio::task::JoinSet;
   75  use tracing::warn;
   76
   77  static LIVE_CONNECTIONS: Gauge = Gauge::new("mcp.connections.live");
   78
   79  pub(crate) struct McpServerConnection {
   80      identity: Option<McpServerConnectionIdentity>,
   81      client: AsyncManagedClient,
   82      startup_trigger: Option<watch::Sender<bool>>,
   83      _diagnostics_guard: GaugeGuard,
   84  }
   85
   86  impl McpServerConnection {
   87      async fn reusable_client(
   88          &self,
   89          desired: &McpServerConnectionIdentity,
   90      ) -> Option<ManagedClient> {
   91          let current = self.identity.as_ref()?;
   92          if !current.has_same_connection_config(desired) {
   93              return None;
   94          }
   95          if !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
   96              return None;
   97          }
   98          let client = self.client.client().await.ok()?;
   99          if client.client.is_closed().await {
  100              return None;
  101          }
  102          if current == desired {
  103              if matches!(desired.oauth_credentials(), Ok(None))
  104                  && tokio::time::timeout(Duration::ZERO, client.client.managed_oauth_credentials())
  105                      .await
  106                      .is_ok_and(|credentials| matches!(credentials, Some(Some(_))))
  107              {
  108                  return None;
  109              }
  110              return Some(client);
  111          }
  112          let Ok(desired_credentials) = desired.oauth_credentials() else {
  113              return Some(client);
  114          };
  115          let reusable = match client.client.managed_oauth_credentials().await {
  116              Some(live_credentials) => live_credentials.as_ref() == desired_credentials,
  117              None => current
  118                  .oauth_credentials()
  119                  .is_ok_and(|startup_credentials| startup_credentials == desired_credentials),
  120          };
  121          if reusable { Some(client) } else { None }
  122      }
  123
  124      pub(crate) async fn client(&self) -> Result<ManagedClient, StartupOutcomeError> {
  125          if let Some(startup_trigger) = &self.startup_trigger {
  126              startup_trigger.send_replace(true);
  127          }
  128          self.client.client().await
  129      }
  130
  131      async fn shutdown(&self) {
  132          self.client.shutdown().await;
  133      }
  134
  135      fn cancel_startup(&self) {
  136          if !self.startup_is_dormant() && !self.client.startup_complete.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
  137              self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  138          }
  139      }
  140
  141      fn startup_is_dormant(&self) -> bool {
  142          self.startup_trigger
  143              .as_ref()
  144              .is_some_and(|startup_trigger| !*startup_trigger.borrow())
  145      }
  146  }
  147
  148  impl Drop for McpServerConnection {
  149      fn drop(&mut self) {
  150          self.client.cancel_token.cancel();
  151      }
  152  }

  164      async fn listed_tools(
  165          &self,
  166          tool_plugin_provenance: &ToolPluginProvenance,
  167      ) -> Option<Vec<ToolInfo>> {
  168          let tools = self.connection.client.listed_tools().await?;
  169          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &self.tool_filter);
  170          Some(if self.connection.client.is_codex_apps_mcp_server {
  171              prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  172          } else {
  173              prepare_regular_mcp_tools_for_model(tools, tool_plugin_provenance)
  174          })
  175      }
  176  }

02
L1factconnectors

02 · 模型只能看到显式可见且能绑定到同一目录版本的 MCP 工具

源码事实

tool_catalog 过滤 UI visibility，不含 metadata 时默认可见；capture_binding 在读锁下捕获 revision、ready client、过滤后的 tools 和 prepared calls，无法精确绑定的工具被省略。

白话解释

展示给模型的工具名和真正执行它的连接必须来自同一版目录，不能拿新版菜单去点旧版厨房。

对自研 Harness 的含义

减少热刷新导致的工具错配和 TOCTOU 风险。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 34–43
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 44–55
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   58      /// Returns all tools with model-visible names normalized.
   59      #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all, fields(mcp_server_count = self.servers.len()))]
   60      pub async fn list_all_tools(&self) -> Vec<ToolInfo> {
   61          let mut tools = Vec::new();
   62          let mut available_server_count = 0;
   63          let mut unavailable_server_count = 0;
   64          for (server_name, view) in &self.servers {
   65              view.connection.client.reconnect_failed_startup().await;
   66              let has_cached_tools = view.connection.client.has_cached_tools();
   67              let startup_complete = view
   68                  .connection
   69                  .client
   70                  .startup_complete
   71                  .load(Ordering::Acquire);
   72              let catalog_override = if server_name == CODEX_APPS_MCP_SERVER_NAME {
   73                  self.codex_apps_tools_override.read().await.clone()
   74              } else {
   75                  None
   76              };
   77              let Some(server_tools) = async {
   78                  match catalog_override {
   79                      Some(tools) => {
   80                          let tools = filter_tools(tools, &view.tool_filter);
   81                          Some(prepare_codex_apps_tools_for_model(
   82                              tools,
   83                              &self.tool_plugin_provenance,
   84                          ))
   85                      }
   86                      None => view.listed_tools(&self.tool_plugin_provenance).await,
   87                  }
   88              }
   89              .instrument(trace_span!(
   90                  "list_tools_for_server",
   91                  server_name = %server_name,
   92                  has_cached_tools,
   93                  startup_complete
   94              ))
   95              .await
   96              else {
   97                  unavailable_server_count += 1;
   98                  trace!(
   99                      server_name = %server_name,
  100                      has_cached_tools,
  101                      startup_complete,
  102                      "MCP server tools unavailable while building tool list"
  103                  );
  104                  continue;
  105              };
  106              available_server_count += 1;
  107              tools.extend(
  108                  server_tools
  109                      .into_iter()
  110                      .map(|tool| Self::with_server_metadata(tool, &view.metadata)),
  111              );
  112          }
  113          let tools = normalize_tools_for_model_with_prefix(
  114              tools,
  115              self.prefix_mcp_tool_names,
  116              &self.non_prefixed_mcp_tool_servers,
  117          );
  118          trace!(
  119              available_server_count,
  120              unavailable_server_count,
  121              tool_count = tools.len(),
  122              "built MCP tool list"
  123          );
  124          tools
  125      }

   62          for (server_name, view) in &self.servers {
   63              if !view
   64                  .connection

07
DIMENSION · SECURITY

安全、审批与权限

4 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L2factsecurity

01 · 审批策略把“何时问”与“允许做什么”分成两条轴

源码事实

AskForApproval 有 UnlessTrusted、OnRequest、Granular 和 Never；SandboxPolicy 独立描述 DangerFullAccess、ReadOnly、ExternalSandbox 与 WorkspaceWrite。

白话解释

一条轴决定要不要敲门，另一条轴决定进门后活动范围；“不用问”不等于“拥有整台机器”。

对自研 Harness 的含义

企业策略能单独收紧提权频率与文件/网络能力。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 890–910
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 911–932
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/protocol/src/protocol.rs:890–932固定提交 ↗
  890  /// Determines the conditions under which the user is consulted to approve
  891  /// running the command proposed by Codex.
  892  #[derive(
  893      Debug,
  894      Clone,
  895      Copy,
  896      Default,
  897      PartialEq,
  898      Eq,
  899      Hash,
  900      Serialize,
  901      Deserialize,
  902      Display,
  903      JsonSchema,
  904      TS,
  905  )]
  906  #[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
  907  #[strum(serialize_all = "kebab-case")]
  908  pub enum AskForApproval {
  909      /// Under this policy, only "known safe" commands—as determined by
  910      /// `is_safe_command()`—that **only read files** are auto‑approved.
  911      /// Everything else will ask the user to approve.
  912      #[serde(rename = "untrusted")]
  913      #[strum(serialize = "untrusted")]
  914      UnlessTrusted,
  915
  916      /// The model decides when to ask the user for approval.
  917      #[serde(alias = "on-failure")]
  918      #[default]
  919      OnRequest,
  920
  921      /// Fine-grained controls for individual approval flows.
  922      ///
  923      /// When a field is `true`, commands in that category are allowed. When it
  924      /// is `false`, those requests are automatically rejected instead of shown
  925      /// to the user.
  926      #[strum(serialize = "granular")]
  927      Granular(GranularApprovalConfig),
  928
  929      /// Never ask the user to approve commands. Failures are immediately returned
  930      /// to the model, and never escalated to the user for approval.
  931      Never,
  932  }
  933
  934  #[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize, JsonSchema, TS)]
  935  pub struct GranularApprovalConfig {
  936      /// Whether to allow shell command approval requests, including inline
  937      /// `with_additional_permissions` and `require_escalated` requests.
  938      pub sandbox_approval: bool,
  939      /// Whether to allow prompts triggered by execpolicy `prompt` rules.
  940      pub rules: bool,
  941      /// Whether to allow approval prompts triggered by skill script execution.
  942      #[serde(default)]
  943      pub skill_approval: bool,
  944      /// Whether to allow prompts triggered by the `request_permissions` tool.
  945      #[serde(default)]
  946      pub request_permissions: bool,
  947      /// Whether to allow MCP elicitation prompts.
  948      pub mcp_elicitations: bool,
  949  }
  950

  895  /// Determines the conditions under which the user is consulted to approve
  896  /// running the command proposed by Codex.
  897  #[derive(
  898      Debug,
  899      Clone,
  900      Copy,
  901      Default,
  902      PartialEq,
  903      Eq,
  904      Hash,
  905      Serialize,
  906      Deserialize,
  907      Display,
  908      JsonSchema,
  909      TS,
  910  )]
  911  #[serde(rename_all = "kebab-case")]
  912  #[strum(serialize_all = "kebab-case")]
  913  pub enum AskForApproval {
  914      /// Under this policy, only "known safe" commands—as determined by
  915      /// `is_safe_command()`—that **only read files** are auto‑approved.
  916      /// Everything else will ask the user to approve.
  917      #[serde(rename = "untrusted")]
  918      #[strum(serialize = "untrusted")]
  919      UnlessTrusted,
  920
  921      /// The model decides when to ask the user for approval.
  922      #[serde(alias = "on-failure")]
  923      #[default]
  924      OnRequest,
  925
  926      /// Fine-grained controls for individual approval flows.
  927      ///
  928      /// When a field is `true`, commands in that category are allowed. When it
  929      /// is `false`, those requests are automatically rejected instead of shown
  930      /// to the user.
  931      #[strum(serialize = "granular")]
  932      Granular(GranularApprovalConfig),
  933
  934      /// Never ask the user to approve commands. Failures are immediately returned
  935      /// to the model, and never escalated to the user for approval.
  936      Never,
  937  }
  938

  948      pub skill_approval: bool,

  950      #[serde(default)]
  951      pub request_permissions: bool,
  952      /// Whether to allow MCP elicitation prompts.
  953      pub mcp_elicitations: bool,
  954  }

02
L1factsecurity

02 · 审批缺失默认中止，且可授予一次、本会话或规则/网络修订

源码事实

命令审批包含 command、cwd、reason、network amendment、execpolicy amendment、additional permissions 和 plugin provenance；等待通道消失时默认 Abort。ReviewDecision 支持单次、session、execpolicy/network amendment、拒绝、超时和 abort。

白话解释

授权不是一个“永远允许”按钮；可以只放这次、放本会话、或把精确规则写进政策，没人回答则停下。

对自研 Harness 的含义

失败关闭且授权可结构化沉淀，适合审计。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 2295–2334
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 2335–2376
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

 2295      pub async fn request_command_approval(
 2296          &self,
 2297          turn_context: &TurnContext,
 2298          call_id: String,
 2299          approval_id: Option<String>,
 2300          environment_id: Option<String>,
 2301          command: Vec<String>,
 2302          cwd: AbsolutePathBuf,
 2303          reason: Option<String>,
 2304          network_approval_context: Option<NetworkApprovalContext>,
 2305          proposed_execpolicy_amendment: Option<ExecPolicyAmendment>,
 2306          additional_permissions: Option<AdditionalPermissionProfile>,
 2307          available_decisions: Option<Vec<ReviewDecision>>,
 2308          plugin_attribution_override: Option<PluginCommandAttribution>,
 2309      ) -> ReviewDecision {
 2310          let _elicitation = self.services.elicitations.register();
 2311          //  command-level approvals use `call_id`.
 2312          // `approval_id` is only present for subcommand callbacks (execve intercept)
 2313          let effective_approval_id = approval_id.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| call_id.clone());
 2314          // Add the tx_approve callback to the map before sending the request.
 2315          let (tx_approve, rx_approve) = oneshot::channel();
 2316          let prev_entry = {
 2317              let mut active = self.active_turn.lock().await;
 2318              match active.as_mut() {
 2319                  Some(at) => {
 2320                      let mut ts = at.turn_state.lock().await;
 2321                      ts.insert_pending_approval(effective_approval_id.clone(), tx_approve)
 2322                  }
 2323                  None => None,
 2324              }
 2325          };
 2326          if prev_entry.is_some() {
 2327              warn!("Overwriting existing pending approval for call_id: {effective_approval_id}");
 2328          }
 2329
 2330          let parsed_cmd = parse_command(&command);
 2331          let proposed_network_policy_amendments = network_approval_context.as_ref().map(|context| {
 2332              vec![
 2333                  NetworkPolicyAmendment {
 2334                      host: context.host.clone(),
 2335                      action: NetworkPolicyRuleAction::Allow,
 2336                  },
 2337                  NetworkPolicyAmendment {
 2338                      host: context.host.clone(),
 2339                      action: NetworkPolicyRuleAction::Deny,
 2340                  },
 2341              ]
 2342          });
 2343          let available_decisions = available_decisions.unwrap_or_else(|| {
 2344              ExecApprovalRequestEvent::default_available_decisions(
 2345                  network_approval_context.as_ref(),
 2346                  proposed_execpolicy_amendment.as_ref(),
 2347                  proposed_network_policy_amendments.as_deref(),
 2348                  additional_permissions.as_ref(),
 2349              )
 2350          });
 2351          let plugin_attribution = plugin_attribution_override
 2352              .or_else(|| turn_context.plugin_attribution_for_command(&command, &cwd));
 2353          let (plugin_id, script_path) = plugin_attribution
 2354              .as_ref()
 2355              .map(PluginCommandAttribution::serialized_fields)
 2356              .unzip();
 2357          let event = EventMsg::ExecApprovalRequest(ExecApprovalRequestEvent {
 2358              call_id,
 2359              plugin_id,
 2360              script_path,
 2361              approval_id,
 2362              turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
 2363              environment_id,
 2364              started_at_ms: now_unix_timestamp_ms(),
 2365              command,
 2366              cwd,
 2367              reason,
 2368              network_approval_context,
 2369              proposed_execpolicy_amendment,
 2370              proposed_network_policy_amendments,
 2371              additional_permissions,
 2372              available_decisions: Some(available_decisions),
 2373              parsed_cmd,
 2374          });
 2375          self.send_event(turn_context, event).await;
 2376          rx_approve.await.unwrap_or(ReviewDecision::Abort)
 2377      }

 2298      fn validated_network_policy_amendment_host(
 2299          amendment: &NetworkPolicyAmendment,
 2300          network_approval_context: &NetworkApprovalContext,
 2301      ) -> anyhow::Result<String> {
 2302          let approved_host = normalize_host(&network_approval_context.host);
 2303          let amendment_host = normalize_host(&amendment.host);
 2304          if amendment_host != approved_host {
 2305              return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
 2306                  "network policy amendment host '{}' does not match approved host '{}'",
 2307                  amendment.host,
 2308                  network_approval_context.host
 2309              ));
 2310          }
 2311          Ok(approved_host)
 2312      }

 2389              ExecApprovalRequestEvent::default_available_decisions(
 2390                  network_approval_context.as_ref(),
 2391                  proposed_execpolicy_amendment.as_ref(),
 2392                  proposed_network_policy_amendments.as_deref(),
 2393                  additional_permissions.as_ref(),
 2394              )
 2395          });
 2396          let plugin_attribution = plugin_attribution_override
 2397              .or_else(|| turn_context.plugin_attribution_for_command(&command, &cwd));
 2398          let (plugin_id, script_path) = plugin_attribution
 2399              .as_ref()
 2400              .map(PluginCommandAttribution::serialized_fields)
 2401              .unzip();
 2402          let event = EventMsg::ExecApprovalRequest(ExecApprovalRequestEvent {
 2403              call_id,
 2404              plugin_id,
 2405              script_path,
 2406              approval_id,
 2407              turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
 2408              environment_id,
 2409              started_at_ms: now_unix_timestamp_ms(),
 2410              command,
 2411              cwd,
 2412              reason,
 2413              network_approval_context,
 2414              proposed_execpolicy_amendment,
 2415              proposed_network_policy_amendments,
 2416              additional_permissions,
 2417              available_decisions: Some(available_decisions),
 2418              parsed_cmd,
 2419          });
 2420          self.send_event(turn_context, event).await;
 2421          rx_approve.await.unwrap_or(ReviewDecision::Abort)
 2422      }
 2423
 2424      #[expect(
 2425          clippy::await_holding_invalid_type,
 2426          reason = "active turn checks and turn state updates must remain atomic"
 2427      )]
 2428      pub async fn request_patch_approval(
 2429          &self,
 2430          turn_context: &TurnContext,
 2431          call_id: String,
 2432          changes: HashMap<PathBuf, FileChange>,
 2433          reason: Option<String>,
 2434          grant_root: Option<PathBuf>,
 2435      ) -> ReviewDecision {
 2436          let _elicitation = self.services.elicitations.register();
 2437          // Add the tx_approve callback to the map before sending the request.
 2438          let (tx_approve, rx_approve) = oneshot::channel();
 2439          let approval_id = call_id.clone();
 2440          let prev_entry = {
 2441              let mut active = self.active_turn.lock().await;
 2442              match active.as_mut() {
 2443                  Some(at) => {
 2444                      let mut ts = at.turn_state.lock().await;
 2445                      ts.insert_pending_approval(approval_id.clone(), tx_approve)
 2446                  }
 2447                  None => None,
03
L1factsecurity

03 · 沙箱按平台变换真实进程：macOS Seatbelt、Linux seccomp/bwrap/landlock、Windows restricted token

源码事实

SandboxManager 将抽象策略映射到平台后端；Auto 只在策略要求时选沙箱，Require/Forbid 明确覆盖选择，随后把命令变换为对应 launcher。

白话解释

不是在提示词里说“请别乱动”，而是在启动进程前给命令套上操作系统能执行的限制器。

对自研 Harness 的含义

强度取决于平台与后端可用性；报告不把 unsupported 平台推断成同等隔离。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 34–52
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 53–73
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   44          match self {
   45              SandboxType::None => "none",
   46              SandboxType::MacosSeatbelt => "seatbelt",
   47              SandboxType::LinuxSeccomp => "seccomp",
   48              SandboxType::WindowsRestrictedToken => "windows_sandbox",
   49          }
   50      }
   51  }

   57      Forbid,
   58  }

   60  pub fn get_platform_sandbox(windows_sandbox_enabled: bool) -> Option<SandboxType> {
   61      if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
   62          Some(SandboxType::MacosSeatbelt)
   63      } else if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
   64          Some(SandboxType::LinuxSeccomp)
   65      } else if cfg!(target_os = "windows") {
   66          if windows_sandbox_enabled {
   67              Some(SandboxType::WindowsRestrictedToken)
   68          } else {
   69              None
   70          }
   71      } else {
   72          None
   73      }
   74  }

   76  pub fn with_managed_mitm_ca_readable_root(
   77      permission_profile: PermissionProfile,
   78      managed_mitm_ca_trust_bundle_path: Option<&AbsolutePathBuf>,
   79      sandbox_policy_cwd: &Path,
   80  ) -> PermissionProfile {
   81      let Some(managed_mitm_ca_trust_bundle_path) = managed_mitm_ca_trust_bundle_path else {
   82          return permission_profile;
   83      };
   84      let (file_system_sandbox_policy, network_sandbox_policy) =
   85          permission_profile.to_runtime_permissions();
   86      let file_system_sandbox_policy = file_system_sandbox_policy.with_additional_readable_roots(
   87          sandbox_policy_cwd,
   88          std::slice::from_ref(managed_mitm_ca_trust_bundle_path),
   89      );
   90      PermissionProfile::from_runtime_permissions_with_enforcement(
   91          permission_profile.enforcement(),
   92          &file_system_sandbox_policy,
   93          network_sandbox_policy,
   94      )
   95  }

04
L1factsecurity

04 · 自定义 permission profile 默认从受限文件系统和受限网络开始

源码事实

custom profile 编译器先建立 restricted filesystem 与 restricted network，再应用条目；网络只有显式 true 才启用，WorkspaceWrite 默认也关闭网络，并保护 .git hooks 等元数据子路径。

白话解释

自定义政策从“什么都别给”开始逐项开门，而不是先全开再查漏补缺。

对自研 Harness 的含义

默认拒绝更适合作为组织级安全基线。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 203–207
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 208–213
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

codex-rs/core/src/config/permissions.rs:203–213固定提交 ↗
  203  fn extensible_builtin_parent_profile(profile_name: &str) -> Option<PermissionProfileToml> {
  204      let file_system = match profile_name {
  205          BUILT_IN_READ_ONLY_PROFILE => FileSystemSandboxPolicy::read_only(),
  206          BUILT_IN_WORKSPACE_PROFILE => FileSystemSandboxPolicy::workspace_write(
  207              &[],
  208              /*exclude_tmpdir_env_var*/ false,
  209              /*exclude_slash_tmp*/ false,
  210          ),
  211          _ => return None,
  212      };
  213      Some(permission_profile_toml_from_file_system_policy(file_system))
  214  }
  215
  216  fn permission_profile_toml_from_file_system_policy(
  217      file_system: FileSystemSandboxPolicy,
  218  ) -> PermissionProfileToml {
  219      let mut filesystem = FilesystemPermissionsToml {
  220          glob_scan_max_depth: file_system.glob_scan_max_depth,
  221          entries: BTreeMap::new(),
  222      };
  223      for entry in file_system.entries {
  224          insert_filesystem_permission_toml(&mut filesystem.entries, entry);
  225      }
  226      PermissionProfileToml {
  227          description: None,
  228          extends: None,
  229          workspace_roots: None,
  230          filesystem: Some(filesystem),
  231          network: None,
  232      }
  233  }
  234
  235  fn insert_filesystem_permission_toml(
  236      entries: &mut BTreeMap<String, FilesystemPermissionToml>,
  237      entry: FileSystemSandboxEntry,
  238  ) {
  239      if entry.skips_missing_path() {
  240          return;
  241      }
  242
  243      match entry.path {
  244          FileSystemPath::Path { path } => {
  245              entries.insert(
  246                  path.into_path_buf().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
  247                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(entry.access),
  248              );
  249          }
  250          FileSystemPath::GlobPattern { pattern } => {
  251              entries.insert(pattern, FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(entry.access));
  252          }
  253          FileSystemPath::Special { value } => {
  254              insert_special_filesystem_permission_toml(entries, value, entry.access);
  255          }
  256      }
  257  }
  258
  259  fn insert_special_filesystem_permission_toml(
  260      entries: &mut BTreeMap<String, FilesystemPermissionToml>,
  261      value: FileSystemSpecialPath,
  262      access: FileSystemAccessMode,
  263  ) {
  264      match value {
  265          FileSystemSpecialPath::Root => {
  266              entries.insert(
  267                  ":root".to_string(),
  268                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  269              );
  270          }
  271          FileSystemSpecialPath::Minimal => {
  272              entries.insert(
  273                  ":minimal".to_string(),
  274                  FilesystemPermissionToml::Access(access),
  275              );
  276          }
  277          FileSystemSpecialPath::ProjectRoots { subpath } => {
  278              insert_scoped_filesystem_permission_toml(
  279                  entries,
  280                  ":workspace_roots".to_string(),
  281                  subpath.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".to_string()),
  282                  access,
  283              );
  284          }

08
DIMENSION · OBSERVABILITY

观测、日志与回放

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factobservability

01 · 会话采用 JSONL rollout 作为事件事实源，后台 writer 支持 persist、flush 与失败记忆

源码事实

RolloutRecorder 将创建/恢复参数与 RolloutItem 送入后台命令队列；writer task 保存 terminal failure，公开 Persist、Flush、Shutdown acknowledgement。

白话解释

先把每一步写成可重放流水账，后台书记员负责落盘；书记员一旦坏掉，后续调用会记得这次故障而不是假装成功。

对自研 Harness 的含义

支持 resume/fork/审计，也给崩溃恢复和一致性测试提供稳定基线。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 93–131
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 132–171
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

  117      AddItems(Vec<RolloutItem>),

  119          ack: oneshot::Sender<std::io::Result<()>>,
  120      },

  122      Flush {
  123          ack: oneshot::Sender<std::io::Result<()>>,

  130  /// Observable state for the background rollout writer task.
  131  struct RolloutWriterTask {
  132      handle: Mutex<Option<JoinHandle<()>>>,
  133      terminal_failure: Mutex<Option<Arc<IoError>>>,
  134  }
  135
  136  impl RolloutWriterTask {
  137      /// Create task observability state before spawning the writer.
  138      fn new() -> Self {
  139          Self {
  140              handle: Mutex::new(None),
  141              terminal_failure: Mutex::new(None),
  142          }
  143      }
  144
  145      /// Store the spawned task handle so it remains owned for the lifetime of recorder clones.
  146      fn set_handle(&self, handle: JoinHandle<()>) {
  147          let mut guard = self
  148              .handle
  149              .lock()
  150              .unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
  151          *guard = Some(handle);
  152      }
  153
  154      /// Remember a terminal task failure for future recorder API calls.
  155      fn mark_failed(&self, err: &IoError) {
  156          let mut guard = self
  157              .terminal_failure
  158              .lock()
  159              .unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
  160          *guard = Some(Arc::new(clone_io_error(err)));
  161      }
  162
  163      /// Return the terminal writer-task failure, if the task exited with an error.
  164      fn terminal_failure(&self) -> Option<IoError> {
  165          let guard = self
  166              .terminal_failure
  167              .lock()
  168              .unwrap_or_else(std::sync::PoisonError::into_inner);
  169          guard.as_ref().map(|err| clone_io_error(err.as_ref()))
  170      }
  171  }
  172
  173  fn clone_io_error(err: &IoError) -> IoError {
  174      IoError::new(err.kind(), err.to_string())
  175  }
  176
  177  impl RolloutRecorderParams {
  178      #[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
  179      pub fn new(
  180          conversation_id: ThreadId,
  181          forked_from_id: Option<ThreadId>,
  182          parent_thread_id: Option<ThreadId>,
  183          source: SessionSource,
  184          thread_source: Option<ThreadSource>,
  185          originator: String,
  186          base_instructions: BaseInstructions,
  187          dynamic_tools: Vec<DynamicToolSpec>,
  188      ) -> Self {
  189          Self::Create {
  190              session_id: conversation_id.into(),
  191              conversation_id,
  192              forked_from_id,
  193              parent_thread_id,
  194              source: Box::new(source),
  195              thread_source,
  196              originator,
  197              base_instructions,
  198              dynamic_tools,
  199              selected_capability_roots: Vec::new(),
  200              multi_agent_version: None,
  201              history_mode: Default::default(),
  202              history_base: None,
  203              subagent_history_start_ordinal: None,
  204              initial_window_id: None,
  205          }
  206      }
  207
  208      pub fn with_session_id(mut self, session_id: SessionId) -> Self {
  209          if let Self::Create { session_id: id, .. } = &mut self {
  210              *id = session_id;
  211          }
  212          self
  213      }
  214
  215      pub fn with_selected_capability_roots(
  216          mut self,
  217          selected_capability_roots: Vec<SelectedCapabilityRoot>,
  218      ) -> Self {
  219          if let Self::Create {
  220              selected_capability_roots: roots,
  221              ..
  222          } = &mut self
  223          {
  224              *roots = selected_capability_roots;
  225          }
  226          self
  227      }
  228
  229      pub fn with_multi_agent_version(
  230          mut self,
  231          multi_agent_version: Option<MultiAgentVersion>,
  232      ) -> Self {
  233          if let Self::Create {
  234              multi_agent_version: version,
  235              ..
  236          } = &mut self

  238              *version = multi_agent_version;
  239          }

  241      }

02
L1factobservability

02 · SQLite 是可查询镜像，并把状态、日志、目标和记忆拆库降低锁竞争

源码事实

state crate 从 JSONL rollout 提取元数据镜像到 SQLite；StateRuntime 分别打开 state、logs、goals、memories 数据库，并明确把日志和分页历史分离以降低锁竞争。

白话解释

流水账负责忠实记录，SQLite 像索引卡片箱，负责快速搜索；不同类型卡片分柜，避免大家抢同一把锁。

对自研 Harness 的含义

兼顾可恢复事件源与 UI 查询性能，代价是要处理 backfill/reconciliation。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–4
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 5–10
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

03
L1factobservability

03 · 观测横跨模型、工具、hooks、MCP、rollout 与 SQLite，不只是一份 CLI 日志

源码事实

模型 client 注入 SessionTelemetry 与 rollout inference trace；工具 runtime 记录 dispatch/handler/total timing；hooks 发开始/完成事件与 duration；MCP 记录 tool list/refresh；state 定义初始化、错误、回填与 fallback 指标。

白话解释

既能看模型这段花了多久，也能拆出排队、工具处理、钩子、连接器和数据库的时间与故障。

对自研 Harness 的含义

适合做端到端性能归因和故障回放，但需要明确遥测数据的隐私与导出策略。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 74–81
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 82–91
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

09
DIMENSION · COLLABORATION

协作与子 Agent

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factcollaboration

01 · 多 Agent 是共享控制面的线程树，不是主循环里的递归函数

源码事实

每棵 root thread 树共享 AgentControl、registry、V2 residency、execution limiter 和 rollout budget；spawn 通过 ThreadManager 创建独立 thread，子线程继承环境与 exec policy。

白话解释

每个子 Agent 都有自己的会话账本，但兄弟们共用一张组织架构表、并发配额和总预算。

对自研 Harness 的含义

隔离了对话状态，同时能做全树限流、恢复和协作观测。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 70–89
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 90–111
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   88
   89  /// Control-plane handle for multi-agent operations.
   90  /// `AgentControl` is held by each session (via `SessionServices`). It provides capability to
   91  /// spawn new agents and the inter-agent communication layer.
   92  /// An `AgentControl` instance is intended to be created at most once per root thread/session
   93  /// tree. That same `AgentControl` is then shared with every sub-agent spawned from that root,
   94  /// which keeps the registry scoped to that root thread rather than the entire `ThreadManager`.
   95  #[derive(Clone, Default)]
   96  pub(crate) struct AgentControl {
   97      /// ID shared by the whole agent control session. This means every sub-agents from a common
   98      /// root share the same session ID.
   99      session_id: SessionId,
  100      /// Weak handle back to the global thread registry/state.
  101      /// This is `Weak` to avoid reference cycles and shadow persistence of the form
  102      /// `ThreadManagerState -> CodexThread -> Session -> SessionServices -> ThreadManagerState`.
  103      manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  104      state: Arc<AgentRegistry>,
  105      v2_residency: Arc<V2Residency>,
  106      agent_execution_limiter: Arc<AgentExecutionLimiter>,
  107      /// Session-scoped state shared by the root thread and every cloned sub-agent control handle.
  108      rollout_budget: Arc<RolloutBudget>,
  109  }
  110
  111  impl AgentControl {
  112      /// Construct a new `AgentControl` that can spawn/message agents via the given manager state.
  113      pub(crate) fn new(
  114          manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  115          rollout_budget: Option<RolloutBudgetConfig>,
  116      ) -> Self {
  117          let control = Self {
  118              manager,
  119              ..Default::default()
  120          };
  121          if let Some(rollout_budget) = rollout_budget {
  122              control.rollout_budget.configure(rollout_budget);
  123          }
  124          control
  125      }
  126
  127      pub(crate) fn with_session_id(mut self, session_id: SessionId, max_threads: usize) -> Self {
  128          self.session_id = session_id;
  129          self.agent_execution_limiter.initialize(max_threads);
  130          self
  131      }
  132
  133      pub(crate) fn session_id(&self) -> SessionId {
  134          self.session_id
  135      }
  136
  137      pub(crate) fn rollout_budget(&self) -> &RolloutBudget {
  138          self.rollout_budget.as_ref()
  139      }
  140
  141      /// Send rich user input items to an existing agent thread.
  142      pub(crate) async fn send_input(
  143          &self,
  144          agent_id: ThreadId,
  145          input: Vec<UserInput>,
  146      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  147          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  148          self.ensure_execution_capacity_for_turn_start(agent_id, /*starts_turn*/ true)
  149              .await?;
  150          self.send_input_after_capacity_check(agent_id, &state, input)
  151              .await
  152      }
  153
  154      async fn send_input_after_capacity_check(
  155          &self,
  156          agent_id: ThreadId,
  157          state: &Arc<ThreadManagerState>,
  158          input: Vec<UserInput>,
  159      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  160          self.handle_thread_request_result(
  161              agent_id,
  162              state,
  163              state.send_op(agent_id, input.into()).await,
  164          )
  165          .await
  166      }
  167
  168      pub(crate) async fn send_inter_agent_communication(
  169          &self,
  170          agent_id: ThreadId,
  171          communication: InterAgentCommunication,
  172          agent_communication_context: AgentCommunicationContext,
  173      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  174          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  175          self.ensure_execution_capacity_for_turn_start(agent_id, communication.trigger_turn)
  176              .await?;
  177          self.send_inter_agent_communication_after_capacity_check(
  178              agent_id,
  179              &state,
  180              communication,
  181              agent_communication_context,
  182          )

   97  /// Control-plane handle for multi-agent operations.
   98  /// `AgentControl` is held by each session (via `SessionServices`). It provides capability to
   99  /// spawn new agents and the inter-agent communication layer.
  100  /// An `AgentControl` instance is intended to be created at most once per root thread/session
  101  /// tree. That same `AgentControl` is then shared with every sub-agent spawned from that root,
  102  /// which keeps the registry scoped to that root thread rather than the entire `ThreadManager`.
  103  #[derive(Clone)]
  104  pub(crate) struct AgentControl {
  105      /// ID shared by the whole agent control session. This means every sub-agents from a common
  106      /// root share the same session ID.
  107      session_id: SessionId,
  108      /// Weak handle back to the global thread registry/state.
  109      /// This is `Weak` to avoid reference cycles and shadow persistence of the form
  110      /// `ThreadManagerState -> CodexThread -> Session -> SessionServices -> ThreadManagerState`.
  111      manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  112      /// Captured at construction so delegates retain their manager's allocation policy.
  113      thread_id_generator: ThreadIdGenerator,
  114      state: Arc<AgentRegistry>,
  115      v2_residency: Arc<V2Residency>,
  116      agent_execution_limiter: Arc<AgentExecutionLimiter>,
  117      /// Session-scoped state shared by the root thread and every cloned sub-agent control handle.
  118      rollout_budget: Arc<RolloutBudget>,
  119  }
  120
  121  impl Default for AgentControl {
  122      fn default() -> Self {
  123          Self::new(
  124              Weak::default(),
  125              default_thread_id_generator(),
  126              /*rollout_budget*/ None,
  127          )
  128      }
  129  }
  130
  131  impl AgentControl {
  132      /// Construct a new `AgentControl` that can spawn/message agents via the given manager state.
  133      pub(crate) fn new(
  134          manager: Weak<ThreadManagerState>,
  135          thread_id_generator: ThreadIdGenerator,
  136          rollout_budget: Option<RolloutBudgetConfig>,
  137      ) -> Self {
  138          let control = Self {
  139              session_id: SessionId::default(),
  140              manager,
  141              thread_id_generator,
  142              state: Arc::default(),
  143              v2_residency: Arc::default(),
  144              agent_execution_limiter: Arc::default(),
  145              rollout_budget: Arc::default(),
  146          };
  147          if let Some(rollout_budget) = rollout_budget {
  148              control.rollout_budget.configure(rollout_budget);
  149          }
  150          control
  151      }
  152
  153      pub(crate) fn with_session_id(mut self, session_id: SessionId, max_threads: usize) -> Self {
  154          self.session_id = session_id;
  155          self.agent_execution_limiter.initialize(max_threads);
  156          self
  157      }
  158
  159      pub(crate) fn session_id(&self) -> SessionId {
  160          self.session_id
  161      }
  162
  163      pub(crate) fn generate_thread_id(&self) -> ThreadId {
  164          (self.thread_id_generator)()
  165      }
  166
  167      pub(crate) fn rollout_budget(&self) -> &RolloutBudget {
  168          self.rollout_budget.as_ref()
  169      }
  170
  171      /// Send rich user input items to an existing agent thread.
  172      pub(crate) async fn send_input(
  173          &self,
  174          agent_id: ThreadId,
  175          input: Vec<UserInput>,
  176          parent_turn_id: Option<String>,
  177          root_turn_id: Option<String>,
  178      ) -> CodexResult<String> {
  179          let state = self.upgrade()?;
  180          let thread = state.get_thread(agent_id).await?;
  181          let result = match thread
  182              .start_or_steer_turn(
02
L1factcollaboration

02 · fork 可选全历史、最近 N 轮或空白；消息可只入队也可触发 turn

源码事实

spawn_agent V2 解析 fork_turns none/all/正整数，默认 all；send_message 只投递队列，followup 类通信可触发空闲 agent 开新 turn，wait 同时监听 mailbox、steer 和 timeout。

白话解释

派工时可把整本案卷、最近几页或一张白纸交给下属；便签可以只塞进邮箱，也可以按门铃让他立即处理。

对自研 Harness 的含义

上下文成本可控，并避免普通消息总是打断正在执行的子任务。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 39–101
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 102–165
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

   39  async fn handle_spawn_agent(
   40      invocation: ToolInvocation,
   41  ) -> Result<SpawnAgentResult, FunctionCallError> {
   42      let ToolInvocation {
   43          session,
   44          turn,
   45          payload,
   46          call_id,
   47          ..
   48      } = invocation;
   49      let arguments = function_arguments(payload)?;
   50      let args: SpawnAgentArgs = parse_arguments(&arguments)?;
   51      let fork_mode = args.fork_mode()?;
   52      let role_name = args
   53          .agent_type
   54          .as_deref()
   55          .map(str::trim)
   56          .filter(|role| !role.is_empty());
   57
   58      let message = message_content(args.message)?;
   59      let session_source = turn.session_source.clone();
   60      let child_depth = next_thread_spawn_depth(&session_source);
   61      let mut config =
   62          build_agent_spawn_config(&session.get_base_instructions().await, turn.as_ref())?;
   63      if let Some(service_tier) = args.service_tier.as_ref() {
   64          config.service_tier = Some(service_tier.clone());
   65      }
   66      let is_full_history_fork = matches!(fork_mode, Some(SpawnAgentForkMode::FullHistory));
   67      if is_full_history_fork {
   68          reject_full_fork_agent_type_override(role_name)?;
   69      }
   70      apply_requested_spawn_agent_model_overrides(
   71          &session,
   72          turn.as_ref(),
   73          &mut config,
   74          args.model.as_deref(),
   75          args.reasoning_effort.clone(),
   76      )
   77      .await?;
   78      if !is_full_history_fork {
   79          apply_spawn_agent_role(&session, &mut config, role_name).await?;
   80      }
   81      apply_spawn_agent_service_tier(
   82          &session,
   83          &mut config,
   84          turn.config.service_tier.as_deref(),
   85          args.service_tier.as_deref(),
   86      )
   87      .await?;
   88      apply_spawn_agent_runtime_overrides(&mut config, turn.as_ref())?;
   89
   90      let spawn_source = thread_spawn_source(
   91          session.thread_id,
   92          &turn.session_source,
   93          child_depth,
   94          role_name,
   95          Some(args.task_name.clone()),
   96      )?;
   97      let new_agent_path = spawn_source.get_agent_path().ok_or_else(|| {
   98          FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
   99              "spawned agent is missing a canonical task name".to_string(),
  100          )
  101      })?;
  102      let author = turn
  103          .session_source
  104          .get_agent_path()
  105          .unwrap_or_else(AgentPath::root);
  106      let communication = communication_from_tool_message(author, new_agent_path.clone(), message);
  107      let context = AgentCommunicationContext::new(AgentCommunicationKind::Spawn, session.thread_id);
  108      let spawned_agent = Box::pin(
  109          session
  110              .services
  111              .agent_control
  112              .spawn_agent_with_communication(
  113                  config,
  114                  communication,
  115                  context,
  116                  Some(spawn_source),
  117                  SpawnAgentOptions {
  118                      fork_parent_spawn_call_id: fork_mode.as_ref().map(|_| call_id.clone()),
  119                      fork_mode,
  120                      parent_thread_id: Some(session.thread_id),
  121                      environments: Some(turn.environments.to_selections()),
  122                  },
  123              ),
  124      )
  125      .await
  126      .map_err(collab_spawn_error)?;
  127      let new_thread_id = spawned_agent.thread_id;
  128      let agent_snapshot = session
  129          .services
  130          .agent_control
  131          .get_agent_config_snapshot(new_thread_id)
  132          .await;
  133      let nickname = agent_snapshot
  134          .as_ref()
  135          .and_then(|snapshot| snapshot.session_source.get_nickname())
  136          .or(spawned_agent.metadata.agent_nickname);
  137      emit_sub_agent_activity(
  138          &session,
  139          &turn,
  140          SubAgentActivityItem {
  141              id: call_id,
  142              agent_thread_id: new_thread_id,
  143              agent_path: new_agent_path.clone(),
  144              kind: SubAgentActivityKind::Started,
  145          },
  146      )
  147      .await;
  148      let role_tag = role_name.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_ROLE_NAME);
  149      turn.session_telemetry.counter(
  150          "codex.multi_agent.spawn",
  151          /*inc*/ 1,
  152          &[("role", role_tag), ("version", "v2")],
  153      );
  154      let task_name = String::from(new_agent_path);
  155
  156      let hide_agent_metadata = turn.config.multi_agent_v2.hide_spawn_agent_metadata;
  157      if hide_agent_metadata {
  158          Ok(SpawnAgentResult::HiddenMetadata { task_name })
  159      } else {
  160          Ok(SpawnAgentResult::WithNickname {
  161              task_name,
  162              nickname,
  163          })
  164      }
  165  }

  168      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
  169          matches!(payload, ToolPayload::Function { .. })

  173  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  174  #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
  175  struct SpawnAgentArgs {
  176      message: String,
  177      task_name: String,
  178      agent_type: Option<String>,
  179      model: Option<String>,
  180      reasoning_effort: Option<ReasoningEffort>,
  181      service_tier: Option<String>,
  182      fork_turns: Option<String>,
  183      fork_context: Option<bool>,
  184  }
  185
  186  impl SpawnAgentArgs {
  187      fn fork_mode(&self) -> Result<Option<SpawnAgentForkMode>, FunctionCallError> {
  188          if self.fork_context.is_some() {
  189              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  190                  "fork_context is not supported in MultiAgentV2; use fork_turns instead".to_string(),
  191              ));
  192          }
  193
  194          let fork_turns = self
  195              .fork_turns
  196              .as_deref()
  197              .map(str::trim)
  198              .filter(|fork_turns| !fork_turns.is_empty())
  199              .unwrap_or("all");
  200
  201          if fork_turns.eq_ignore_ascii_case("none") {
  202              return Ok(None);
  203          }
  204          if fork_turns.eq_ignore_ascii_case("all") {
  205              return Ok(Some(SpawnAgentForkMode::FullHistory));
  206          }
  207
  208          let last_n_turns = fork_turns.parse::<usize>().map_err(|_| {
  209              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  210                  "fork_turns must be `none`, `all`, or a positive integer string".to_string(),
  211              )
  212          })?;
  213          if last_n_turns == 0 {
  214              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  215                  "fork_turns must be `none`, `all`, or a positive integer string".to_string(),
  216              ));
  217          }
  218
  219          Ok(Some(SpawnAgentForkMode::LastNTurns(last_n_turns)))
  220      }
  221  }
  222
  223  #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]
  224  #[serde(untagged)]
  225  pub(crate) enum SpawnAgentResult {
  226      WithNickname {
  227          task_name: String,
  228          nickname: Option<String>,
  229      },
  230      HiddenMetadata {
  231          task_name: String,
  232      },
  233  }

  236      fn log_preview(&self) -> String {

  162              agent_path: new_agent_path.clone(),
  163              kind: SubAgentActivityKind::Started,
  164          },
  165      )
  166      .await;
  167      let role_tag = role_name.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_ROLE_NAME);
  168      turn.session_telemetry.counter(
  169          "codex.multi_agent.spawn",
  170          /*inc*/ 1,
  171          &[("role", role_tag), ("version", "v2")],
  172      );
  173      let task_name = String::from(new_agent_path);
  174
  175      let hide_agent_metadata = turn.config.multi_agent_v2.hide_spawn_agent_metadata;
  176      if hide_agent_metadata {
  177          Ok(SpawnAgentResult::HiddenMetadata { task_name })
  178      } else {
  179          Ok(SpawnAgentResult::WithNickname {
  180              task_name,
  181              nickname,
  182          })
  183      }
  184  }
  185
  186  impl CoreToolRuntime for Handler {
  187      fn matches_kind(&self, payload: &ToolPayload) -> bool {
  188          matches!(payload, ToolPayload::Function { .. })
  189      }
  190  }
  191
  192  #[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
  193  #[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
  194  struct SpawnAgentArgs {
  195      message: String,
  196      task_name: String,
  197      agent_type: Option<String>,
  198      model: Option<String>,
  199      reasoning_effort: Option<ReasoningEffort>,
  200      service_tier: Option<String>,
  201      fork_turns: Option<String>,
  202      fork_context: Option<bool>,
  203  }
  204
  205  impl SpawnAgentArgs {
  206      fn fork_mode(&self) -> Result<Option<SpawnAgentForkMode>, FunctionCallError> {
  207          if self.fork_context.is_some() {
  208              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  209                  "fork_context is not supported in MultiAgentV2; use fork_turns instead".to_string(),
  210              ));
  211          }
  212
  213          let fork_turns = self
  214              .fork_turns
  215              .as_deref()
  216              .map(str::trim)
  217              .filter(|fork_turns| !fork_turns.is_empty())
  218              .unwrap_or("all");
  219
  220          if fork_turns.eq_ignore_ascii_case("none") {
  221              return Ok(None);
  222          }
  223          if fork_turns.eq_ignore_ascii_case("all") {
  224              return Ok(Some(SpawnAgentForkMode::FullHistory));
  225          }
  226
  227          let last_n_turns = fork_turns.parse::<usize>().map_err(|_| {
  228              FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  229                  "fork_turns must be `none`, `all`, or a positive integer string".to_string(),
  230              )
  231          })?;
  232          if last_n_turns == 0 {
  233              return Err(FunctionCallError::RespondToModel(
  234                  "fork_turns must be `none`, `all`, or a positive integer string".to_string(),
  235              ));
  236          }
03
L1factcollaboration

03 · 驻留上限与同时执行上限分离，V2 子 Agent 才受执行 limiter

源码事实

V2 的 effective_agent_max_threads 从 max_concurrent_threads_per_session 减去 root；AgentExecutionLimiter 只在 V2 SubAgent 启动新 turn 时检查 active 数，已有 active turn 的消息不重复占槽，guard drop 释放名额。

白话解释

可以让许多子会话留在通讯录里，但只有有限几个同时开工；已经在干活的人收到补充便签不会再算一个工位。

对自研 Harness 的含义

资源治理比单一 agent count 精细，恢复/驻留和运行并发可以独立调优。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1547–1552
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 1553–1560
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

10
DIMENSION · INSTRUCTIONS

全部指令与扩展面

3 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L1factinstructions

01 · AGENTS.md 从项目根向 cwd 分层合并，局部 override 优先且有总字节预算

源码事实

发现逻辑以 project root marker 截断上行范围，从根到 cwd 每层最多选 AGENTS.override.md、AGENTS.md 或 fallback 文件之一；按总预算读取，超出部分截断。

白话解释

公司规章先读，走进子目录后再叠加本地规章；同一层的 override 像贴在门上的最新通知。

对自研 Harness 的含义

目录作用域明确，但超预算时越靠后的深层说明更可能被截断，需要可视化告警。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–7
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 8–16
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

    1  //! AGENTS.md discovery and user instruction assembly.
    2  //!
    3  //! Project-level documentation is primarily stored in files named `AGENTS.md`.
    4  //! Additional fallback filenames can be configured via `project_doc_fallback_filenames`.
    5  //! We include the concatenation of all files found along the path from the
    6  //! project root to the current working directory as follows:
    7  //!
    8  //! 1.  Determine the project root by walking upwards from the current working
    9  //!     directory until a configured `project_root_markers` entry is found.
   10  //!     When `project_root_markers` is unset, the default marker list is used
   11  //!     (`.git`). If no marker is found, only the current working directory is
   12  //!     considered. An empty marker list disables parent traversal.
   13  //! 2.  Collect every `AGENTS.md` found from the project root down to the
   14  //!     current working directory (inclusive) and concatenate their contents in
   15  //!     that order.
   16  //! 3.  We do **not** walk past the project root.

   46  // Metadata probes are cheap and the exec-server transport already bounds total in-flight calls.

   48  // transport when independent startup discovery runs concurrently.

   51  /// Loads project AGENTS.md content and combines it with host-provided user
   52  /// instructions.
   53  pub(crate) async fn load_project_instructions(
   54      config: &Config,
   55      user_instructions: Option<UserInstructions>,
   56      environments: &TurnEnvironmentSnapshot,
   57  ) -> Option<LoadedAgentsMd> {
   58      let mut loaded = LoadedAgentsMd::from_user_instructions(user_instructions);
   59      for turn_environment in environments.turn_environments() {
   60          let filesystem = turn_environment.environment.get_filesystem();
   61          match read_agents_md(
   62              config,
   63              filesystem.as_ref(),
   64              &turn_environment.environment_id,
   65              turn_environment.cwd(),
   66          )
   67          .await
   68          {
   69              Ok(Some(docs)) => loaded.entries.extend(docs.entries),
   70              Ok(None) => {}
   71              Err(e) => {
   72                  error!(
   73                      environment_id = turn_environment.environment_id,
   74                      "error trying to find AGENTS.md docs: {e:#}"
   75                  );
   76              }
   77          }
   78      }
   79
   80      (!loaded.is_empty()).then_some(loaded)
   81  }

   87  /// `Ok(None)`. Unexpected I/O failures bubble up as `Err` so callers can

    8  //! 1.  Determine the project root by walking upwards from the current working
    9  //!     directory until a configured `project_root_markers` entry is found.
   10  //!     When `project_root_markers` is unset, the default marker list is used
   11  //!     (`.git`). If no marker is found, only the current working directory is
   12  //!     considered. An empty marker list disables parent traversal.
   13  //! 2.  Collect every `AGENTS.md` found from the project root down to the
   14  //!     current working directory (inclusive) and concatenate their contents in
   15  //!     that order.
   16  //! 3.  We do **not** walk past the project root.
   17
   18  use crate::config::Config;
   19  use crate::context::UserInstructions as ContextUserInstructions;
   20  use crate::environment_selection::TurnEnvironmentSnapshot;
   21  use codex_config::ConfigLayerSource;
   22  use codex_config::default_project_root_markers;
   23  use codex_config::merge_toml_values;
   24  use codex_config::project_root_markers_from_config;
   25  use codex_exec_server::ExecutorFileSystem;
   26  use codex_extension_api::UserInstructions;
   27  use codex_file_system::FindUpErrorPolicy;
   28  use codex_file_system::find_nearest_ancestor_with_markers;
   29  use codex_utils_absolute_path::AbsolutePathBuf;
   30  use codex_utils_path_uri::PathUri;
   31  use futures::StreamExt;
   32  use std::io;
   33  use toml::Value as TomlValue;
   34  use tracing::error;
   35
   36  /// Default filename scanned for AGENTS.md instructions.
   37  pub const DEFAULT_AGENTS_MD_FILENAME: &str = "AGENTS.md";
   38  /// Preferred local override for AGENTS.md instructions.
   39  pub const LOCAL_AGENTS_MD_FILENAME: &str = "AGENTS.override.md";
   40
   41  /// When both user and project AGENTS.md docs are present, they will be
   42  /// concatenated with the following separator.
   43  const AGENTS_MD_SEPARATOR: &str = "\n\n--- project-doc ---\n\n";
   44
   45  // Metadata probes are cheap and the exec-server transport already bounds total in-flight calls.
   46  // This covers typical project hierarchies in one remote round trip without monopolizing that
   47  // transport when independent startup discovery runs concurrently.
   48  const MAX_CONCURRENT_ANCESTOR_PROBES: usize = 256;
   49
   50  /// Loads project AGENTS.md content and combines it with host-provided user
   51  /// instructions.
   52  pub(crate) async fn load_project_instructions(
   53      config: &Config,
   54      user_instructions: Option<UserInstructions>,
   55      environments: &TurnEnvironmentSnapshot,
   56  ) -> Option<LoadedAgentsMd> {
   57      let mut loaded = LoadedAgentsMd::from_user_instructions(user_instructions);
   58      let mut remaining = config.project_doc_max_bytes;
   59      for turn_environment in environments.turn_environments() {
   60          if remaining == 0 {
   61              break;
   62          }
   63
   64          let filesystem = turn_environment.environment.get_filesystem();
   65          match read_agents_md(
   66              config,
   67              filesystem.as_ref(),
   68              &turn_environment.environment_id,
   69              turn_environment.cwd(),
   70              remaining,
   71          )
   72          .await
   73          {
   74              Ok(Some(docs)) => {
   75                  for entry in docs.entries {
   76                      remaining = remaining.saturating_sub(entry.contents.len());
   77                      loaded.entries.push(entry);
   78                  }
   79              }
   80              Ok(None) => {}
   81              Err(e) => {
   82                  error!(
   83                      environment_id = turn_environment.environment_id,
   84                      "error trying to find AGENTS.md docs: {e:#}"
   85                  );
   86              }
   87          }
02
L1factinstructions

02 · Skills、plugins 和 extensions 都在初始上下文构建期受预算与来源控制

源码事实

build_initial_context 按 context window 计算 skill metadata budget，超限发 warning；plugins 先按配置加载，再生成推荐插件上下文；extension contributors 可注入 developer、contextual user 或独立 developer fragment。

白话解释

扩展不是把所有说明一股脑塞进提示词，而是先做预算，再按可信来源和消息层级装配。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可扩展性强且照顾 prompt cache；插件推荐与已安装插件被区分。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 3336–3365
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 3366–3397
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

L1factinstructions

03 · Hooks 覆盖 session、prompt、permission、tool、compact、stop 与 subagent 生命周期

源码事实

runtime 为 session/subagent start、PreToolUse、PermissionRequest、PostToolUse、UserPromptSubmit 等构造稳定 payload；hook 可阻断、改写输入或注入额外上下文，完成事件同时进入指标和 analytics。

白话解释

钩子既能当门卫，也能当翻译器和旁路记录员；每次执行都有开始、结束和耗时记录。

对自研 Harness 的含义

组织可插入治理逻辑，但 hook 本身应被当作有权限的代码并受项目信任策略保护。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 103–160
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 161–220
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

叙述来自历史证据账本 · 3418498f0142 · 打开账本提交 ↗
codex-rs/core/src/hook_runtime.rs:103–220固定提交 ↗
  103  pub(crate) async fn run_pending_session_start_hooks(
  104      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  105      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  106  ) -> bool {
  107      while let Some(session_start_source) = sess.take_pending_session_start_source().await {
  108          // Pending session-start hooks are reused to dispatch thread-spawn subagent
  109          // starts. Other subagent sessions are internal/system work and do not run
  110          // start hooks.
  111          let target = match &turn_context.session_source {
  112              SessionSource::SubAgent(SubAgentSource::ThreadSpawn { agent_role, .. })
  113                  if matches!(
  114                      session_start_source,
  115                      codex_hooks::SessionStartSource::Startup
  116                  ) =>
  117              {
  118                  let context = subagent_hook_context(sess, agent_role);
  119                  StartHookTarget::SubagentStart {
  120                      turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  121                      agent_id: context.agent_id,
  122                      agent_type: context.agent_type,
  123                  }
  124              }
  125              SessionSource::SubAgent(_) => return false,
  126              _ => StartHookTarget::SessionStart {
  127                  source: session_start_source,
  128              },
  129          };
  130          let request = codex_hooks::SessionStartRequest {
  131              session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  132              #[allow(deprecated)]
  133              cwd: turn_context.cwd.clone(),
  134              transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  135              model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  136              permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  137              target,
  138          };
  139          let hooks = sess.hooks();
  140          let preview_runs = hooks.preview_session_start(&request);
  141          if run_context_injecting_hook(
  142              sess,
  143              turn_context,
  144              preview_runs,
  145              hooks.run_session_start(request, Some(turn_context.sub_id.clone())),
  146          )
  147          .await
  148          .record_additional_contexts(sess, turn_context)
  149          .await
  150          {
  151              return true;
  152          }
  153      }
  154
  155      false
  156  }
  157
  158  /// Runs matching `PreToolUse` hooks before a tool executes.
  159  ///
  160  /// `tool_name` is the canonical name serialized to hook stdin. Matcher aliases
  161  /// are internal compatibility names used only for selecting configured hook
  162  /// handlers.
  163  pub(crate) async fn run_pre_tool_use_hooks(
  164      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  165      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  166      tool_use_id: String,
  167      tool_name: &HookToolName,
  168      tool_input: &Value,
  169  ) -> PreToolUseHookResult {
  170      let request = PreToolUseRequest {
  171          session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  172          turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  173          subagent: thread_spawn_subagent_hook_context(sess, turn_context),
  174          #[allow(deprecated)]
  175          cwd: turn_context.cwd.clone(),
  176          transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  177          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  178          permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  179          tool_name: tool_name.name().to_string(),
  180          matcher_aliases: tool_name.matcher_aliases().to_vec(),
  181          tool_use_id,
  182          tool_input: tool_input.clone(),
  183      };
  184      let hooks = sess.hooks();
  185      let preview_runs = hooks.preview_pre_tool_use(&request);
  186      emit_hook_started_events(sess, turn_context, preview_runs).await;
  187
  188      let PreToolUseOutcome {
  189          hook_events,
  190          should_block,
  191          block_reason,
  192          additional_contexts,
  193          updated_input,
  194      } = hooks.run_pre_tool_use(request).await;
  195      emit_hook_completed_events(sess, turn_context, hook_events).await;
  196      record_additional_contexts(sess, turn_context, additional_contexts).await;
  197
  198      if !should_block {
  199          return PreToolUseHookResult::Continue { updated_input };
  200      }
  201
  202      let Some(reason) = block_reason else {
  203          return PreToolUseHookResult::Continue {
  204              updated_input: None,
  205          };
  206      };
  207
  208      if (tool_name.name() == "Bash" || tool_name.name() == "apply_patch")
  209          && let Some(command) = tool_input.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str)
  210      {
  211          PreToolUseHookResult::Blocked(format!(
  212              "Command blocked by PreToolUse hook: {reason}. Command: {command}"
  213          ))
  214      } else {
  215          PreToolUseHookResult::Blocked(format!(
  216              "Tool call blocked by PreToolUse hook: {reason}. Tool: {}",
  217              tool_name.name()
  218          ))
  219      }
  220  }
  221
  222  // PermissionRequest hooks share the same preview/start/completed event flow as
  223  // other hook types, but they return an optional decision instead of mutating
  224  // tool input or post-run state.
  225  pub(crate) async fn run_permission_request_hooks(
  226      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  227      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  228      run_id_suffix: &str,
  229      payload: PermissionRequestPayload,
  230  ) -> Option<PermissionRequestDecision> {
  231      let request = PermissionRequestRequest {
  232          session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  233          turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  234          subagent: thread_spawn_subagent_hook_context(sess, turn_context),
  235          #[allow(deprecated)]
  236          cwd: turn_context.cwd.to_path_buf(),
  237          transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  238          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  239          permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  240          tool_name: payload.tool_name.name().to_string(),
  241          matcher_aliases: payload.tool_name.matcher_aliases().to_vec(),
  242          run_id_suffix: run_id_suffix.to_string(),
  243          tool_input: payload.tool_input,
  244      };
  245      let hooks = sess.hooks();
  246      let preview_runs = hooks.preview_permission_request(&request);
  247      emit_hook_started_events(sess, turn_context, preview_runs).await;
  248
  249      let PermissionRequestOutcome {
  250          hook_events,
  251          decision,
  252      } = hooks.run_permission_request(request).await;
  253      emit_hook_completed_events(sess, turn_context, hook_events).await;
  254
  255      decision
  256  }

  258  /// Runs matching `PostToolUse` hooks after a tool has produced a successful output.

  261  /// already adapted by the tool handler into the stable hook contract. Passing
  262  /// raw internal tool data here would leak implementation details into user hook
  263  /// matchers and hook logs.
  264  pub(crate) async fn run_post_tool_use_hooks(
  265      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  266      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  267      tool_use_id: String,
  268      tool_name: String,
  269      matcher_aliases: Vec<String>,
  270      tool_input: Value,
  271      tool_response: Value,
  272  ) -> PostToolUseOutcome {
  273      let request = PostToolUseRequest {
  274          session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  275          turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  276          subagent: thread_spawn_subagent_hook_context(sess, turn_context),
  277          #[allow(deprecated)]
  278          cwd: turn_context.cwd.clone(),
  279          transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  280          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  281          permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  282          tool_name,
  283          matcher_aliases,
  284          tool_use_id,
  285          tool_input,
  286          tool_response,
  287      };
  288      let hooks = sess.hooks();
  289      let preview_runs = hooks.preview_post_tool_use(&request);
  290      emit_hook_started_events(sess, turn_context, preview_runs).await;
  291
