  105
  106  #[instrument(level = "trace", skip_all)]
  107  pub(crate) async fn run_pending_session_start_hooks(
  108      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  109      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  110  ) -> bool {
  111      while let Some(session_start_source) = sess.take_pending_session_start_source().await {
  112          // Pending session-start hooks are reused to dispatch thread-spawn subagent
  113          // starts. Other subagent sessions are internal/system work and do not run
  114          // start hooks.
  115          let target = match &turn_context.session_source {
  116              SessionSource::SubAgent(SubAgentSource::ThreadSpawn { agent_role, .. })
  117                  if matches!(
  118                      session_start_source,
  119                      codex_hooks::SessionStartSource::Startup
  120                  ) =>
  121              {
  122                  let context = subagent_hook_context(sess, agent_role);
  123                  StartHookTarget::SubagentStart {
  124                      turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  125                      agent_id: context.agent_id,
  126                      agent_type: context.agent_type,
  127                  }
  128              }
  129              SessionSource::SubAgent(_) => return false,
  130              _ => StartHookTarget::SessionStart {
  131                  source: session_start_source,
  132              },
  133          };
  134          let request = codex_hooks::SessionStartRequest {
  135              session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  136              #[allow(deprecated)]
  137              cwd: turn_context.cwd.clone(),
  138              transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  139              model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  140              permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  141              target,
  142          };
  143          let hooks = sess.hooks();
  144          let preview_runs = hooks.preview_session_start(&request);
  145          if run_context_injecting_hook(
  146              sess,
  147              turn_context,
  148              preview_runs,
  149              hooks.run_session_start(request, Some(turn_context.sub_id.clone())),
  150          )
  151          .await
  152          .record_additional_contexts(sess, turn_context)
  153          .await
  154          {
  155              return true;
  156          }
  157      }
  158
  159      false
  160  }
  161
  162  /// Runs matching `PreToolUse` hooks before a tool executes.
  163  ///
  164  /// `tool_name` is the canonical name serialized to hook stdin. Matcher aliases
  165  /// are internal compatibility names used only for selecting configured hook
  166  /// handlers.
  167  pub(crate) async fn run_pre_tool_use_hooks(
  168      sess: &Arc<Session>,
  169      turn_context: &Arc<TurnContext>,
  170      tool_use_id: String,
  171      tool_name: &HookToolName,
  172      tool_input: &Value,
  173  ) -> PreToolUseHookResult {
  174      let request = PreToolUseRequest {
  175          session_id: sess.session_id().into(),
  176          turn_id: turn_context.sub_id.clone(),
  177          subagent: thread_spawn_subagent_hook_context(sess, turn_context),
  178          #[allow(deprecated)]
  179          cwd: turn_context.cwd.clone(),
  180          transcript_path: sess.hook_transcript_path().await,
  181          model: turn_context.model_info.slug.clone(),
  182          permission_mode: hook_permission_mode(turn_context),
  183          tool_name: tool_name.name().to_string(),
  184          matcher_aliases: tool_name.matcher_aliases().to_vec(),
  185          tool_use_id,
  186          tool_input: tool_input.clone(),
  187      };
  188      let hooks = sess.hooks();
  189      let preview_runs = hooks.preview_pre_tool_use(&request);
  190      emit_hook_started_events(sess, turn_context, preview_runs).await;
  191
  192      let PreToolUseOutcome {
  193          hook_events,
  194          should_block,
  195          block_reason,
  196          additional_contexts,
  197          updated_input,
  198      } = hooks.run_pre_tool_use(request).await;
  199      emit_hook_completed_events(sess, turn_context, hook_events).await;
  200      record_additional_contexts(sess, turn_context, additional_contexts).await;
  201
  202      if !should_block {
  203          return PreToolUseHookResult::Continue { updated_input };
  204      }
  205
  206      let Some(reason) = block_reason else {
  207          return PreToolUseHookResult::Continue {
  208              updated_input: None,
  209          };
  210      };
  211
  212      if (tool_name.name() == "Bash" || tool_name.name() == "apply_patch")
  213          && let Some(command) = tool_input.get("command").and_then(Value::as_str)
  214      {
  215          PreToolUseHookResult::Blocked(format!(
  216              "Command blocked by PreToolUse hook: {reason}. Command: {command}"
  217          ))
  218      } else {
  219          PreToolUseHookResult::Blocked(format!(
  220              "Tool call blocked by PreToolUse hook: {reason}. Tool: {}",
  221              tool_name.name()
  222          ))
  223      }
  224  }

DIMENSION · RECOVERY

恢复、测试与取舍

1 条可定位结论；每条按事实 → 白话 → 工程影响 → 源码摘录展开。

01
L3factrecovery

01 · 这是大型、多前端、测试密集的 Rust Harness，许可证为 Apache-2.0

源码事实

固定快照含约 2,780 个 Rust 文件、645 个 TypeScript 文件；源码树中约 487 个 test 命名文件、1,170 个含 Rust test attribute 的文件，根许可证为 Apache License 2.0。

白话解释

它不是一段 CLI 脚本，而是一套带协议、TUI、app server、状态库、插件和跨平台后端的系统工程。

对自研 Harness 的含义

可借鉴性高，但直接复刻意味着承担很大的平台与回归测试成本。

逐段阅读提示
  1. 1–13
    入口、配置和状态初始化

    先确认谁拿到输入、读取了哪些配置、创建了哪些状态。

  2. 14–28
    分支、副作用和回执

    再追踪条件分支、外部调用、事件写入和失败出口；不要只看函数名。

  3. after
    回到调用者

    最后沿着调用者和下一条事件继续读，确认这段代码的结果如何被消费。

边界 / 风险
  • 文件数与测试文件数是对该固定 checkout 的机械统计，不等于测试覆盖率。
